### [1] [WaveOp-LiteFM: Lightweight Neural-Operator Flow Matching for Satellite-to-Radar Precipitation Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25818)
*Chunlei Shi,Yecheng Zhang,Yufeng Zhu,Dan Niu,Yichao Dong,Yongchao Feng,Junming Hou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出WaveOp-LiteFM，一种基于神经算子流匹配的轻量级卫星到雷达降水反演框架，通过光谱-局部-小波（SLW）块在像素空间中实现高效流匹配，在SEVIR和中国东南数据集上达到最先进性能并大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成流匹配模型在S2R反演中面临两难：像素空间公式因注意力U-Net速度网络计算成本过高，而潜在空间建模会牺牲降水细节或难以处理稀疏目标。需要一种既能保持像素空间高保真度又能显著降低计算负担的方法。

Method: 提出了WaveOp-LiteFM框架，核心是光谱-局部-小波（SLW）块作为速度骨干网络。SLW块将降水特征分解为三个频率域：光谱分支捕获大尺度层状组织，局部分支建模短程交互，小波分支增强尖锐结构并抑制高频噪声。引入输入自适应门控机制动态融合三分支特征，并通过跳跃门在解码器中以相加融合方式整合编码器特征，避免传统U-Net的高成本通道拼接。

Result: 在SEVIR和中国东南数据集上的实验表明，WaveOp-LiteFM实现了最先进的反演性能，同时显著降低了计算成本。此外，在中国大范围推理测试（包括台风巴威案例）中展现了可靠的检索质量。

Conclusion: WaveOp-LiteFM成功解决了像素空间流匹配计算成本高与潜在空间细节丢失之间的矛盾，为有限雷达覆盖区域的降水监测提供了一种高效且高质量的解决方案，并在大规模真实场景中验证了其可靠性和实用性。

Abstract: Satellite-to-radar (S2R) retrieval refers to estimating ground-based radar precipitation from geostationary satellite observations, enabling precipitation monitoring in regions with limited radar coverage. While recent generative flow matching models have greatly advanced retrieval quality, they face a critical trade-off: pixel-space formulations suffer from the prohibitive computational costs of attention-based U-Net velocity networks, whereas latent-space modeling often sacrifices fine precipitation details or struggles with sparse targets. To address this dilemma, we propose WaveOp-LiteFM, a lightweight neural operator flow matching framework for S2R retrieval. Our approach introduces a novel velocity backbone built upon the spectral-local-wavelet (SLW) block, enabling efficient and stable flow matching in pixel space. Specifically, the SLW block disentangles precipitation features into three distinct frequency regimes: (i) the spectral branch captures large-scale stratiform organization; (ii) the local branch models short-range interactions; and (iii) the wavelet branch enhances sharp structures while suppressing noisy high-frequency responses. Building on this design, an input-adaptive gating mechanism dynamically fuses features from the three functional branches. Furthermore, a skip gate efficiently reintegrates encoder features through additive fusion within the decoder, avoiding the costly channel concatenation used in conventional U-Net architectures. Experiments on the SEVIR and Southeast China datasets show that WaveOp-LiteFM achieves state-of-the-art retrieval performance while substantially reducing computational costs. Beyond benchmark evaluation, large-area inference over China, including a recent Typhoon Bavi case, demonstrates that WaveOp-LiteFM maintains reliable retrieval quality in large-scale real-world scenarios.

</details>

### [2] [Analyzing and Reducing Search Quality Differences in Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25185)
*Sara Mahdizadeh Shahri,Martin Prammer,Jignesh M. Patel,Akshitha Sriraman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出RCheck，一个轻量级运行时系统，用于检测和减少近似最近邻搜索中跨查询的召回率差异，通过动态调整每个查询的搜索努力量，在保持高吞吐的同时使更多查询达到目标召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库服务通过近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）在搜索性能和召回率之间权衡，但优化平均召回率会掩盖查询之间的显著召回率差异，导致部分查询低于目标召回率（损害用户体验）而部分查询高于目标召回率（浪费计算资源），因此需要检测并减少跨查询的召回率差异。

Method: RCheck采用动态自适应的核心设计原则：对低于目标召回率的查询增加搜索努力，对高于目标召回率的查询减少搜索努力，通过调整可用的搜索努力参数来实现，使系统易于部署。

Result: 在广泛使用的生产级pgvector数据库上评估，在相同吞吐量下，RCheck相比最先进的全局调优配置，平均召回率提升11-93%，使8-47%更多的查询达到目标召回率。

Conclusion: RCheck通过逐查询动态调整搜索努力，有效减少了召回率差异，在保持高吞吐的同时提升了平均召回率并增加达成目标召回率的查询数量，是一种实用且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern database services scalably search over large data collections via Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search, which improves search performance at the cost of search quality, measured by recall. In practice, a database operator seeks to achieve a target mean recall while maximizing throughput across search queries. We show that optimizing for mean recall masks significant differences in recall across queries even when target recall is met. As a result, numerous queries face (1) below-target recall, hurting user experience and revenue and (2) above-target recall, wasting computation to deliver unnecessarily high search quality. Thus, it is critical to detect and reduce recall differences across queries. We design RCheck, a light-weight run-time system that identifies low-recall queries and reduces recall differences while achieving high throughput. RCheck's key design principle is to dynamically, efficiently adapt search effort by increasing effort for queries below target recall and decreasing effort for those above it. RCheck tunes available search effort parameters, making it readily deployable. We evaluate RCheck using the widely-used production-style pgvector database. At the same throughput, RCheck improves mean recall by 11-93% and enables 8-47% more queries to meet target recall compared to the state-of-the-art globally-tuned configuration.

</details>


### [3] [Bolt-on, Verifiable Provenance for LLM-Powered Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25210)
*Yiming Lin,Sepanta Zeighami,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出BLIP框架，用于为LLM数据处理任务高效推断可验证的、最小的来源（provenance），确保子集输入能复现与完整输入相同的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM是强大的数据处理工具，但作为黑盒，无法提供答案来源或可信度的保证。现有启发式方法（如嵌入相似度或直接询问LLM）只能提供线索，无法保证通过识别出的来源能推导出答案，且常常不准确。

Method: 提出"可验证来源"概念，即识别一个能复现与完整文本相同（或等价）答案的输入子集，并引入最小性概念。为避免穷举所有子集的昂贵方案，设计BLIP框架，包含八种保证能找到最小可验证来源的策略，以及一种自适应策略组合各策略优势降低成本，并扩展支持生成多个最小可验证来源。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，BLIP生成的来源始终保证能复现答案，相比最优基线在可比来源大小下准确率提升超过30%，且成本低廉，与在原始数据上直接查询相当。

Conclusion: BLIP提供了一种高效、有理论保证的LLM来源追踪方案，能够为任意LLM驱动的数据处理任务找到最小可验证来源，兼具高准确率和低成本优势。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful tools for processing data. However, LLMs are also complex black-boxes, returning answers to queries on data,
  without any indication for where the answer came from or whether it is trustworthy. We introduce the notion of provenance for data processing with LLMs. While
  existing heuristics (such as embedding similarity or directly asking an LLM) could provide some hints for where the answer was derived, they provide no guarantees
  that the answer can be derived using the identified provenance, and indeed, are often incorrect. Instead, we propose the notion of verifiable provenance wherein we
  identify a subset of the input text that reproduces the same (or equivalent) answer as that on the complete text, and introduce the notion of minimality, where the
  verifiable provenance is as small as possible. To identify such a provenance, a naive solution would require checking all possible subsets of the source data with the
  LLM, which is prohibitively expensive. We present BLIP, a bolt-on framework for efficiently inferring a small-sized verifiable provenance for any LLM-powered data
  processing task, with any LLM. As part of BLIP, we introduce eight strategies, each guaranteed to find a minimal verifiable provenance, as well as an adaptive
  strategy that combines their strengths to reduce cost further. We further extend BLIP to produce multiple minimal verifiable provenances. Experiments on seven
  datasets show that the provenance generated by BLIP is always guaranteed to reproduce the answer - achieving over 30% higher accuracy than the best-performing
  baseline with a comparable provenance size. Moreover, BLIP incurs a low cost, comparable to the original query on the original data.

</details>


### [4] [DBcover: A White-box SQL Test Generation Framework for Coverage Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25573)
*Yankai Rong,Shuang Liu,Jinhao Dong,Qiang Yin,Wei Lu,Jianhua Wang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DBcover是一个基于LLM的白盒SQL测试生成框架，利用上下文推理来提升RDBMS测试覆盖率，在PostgreSQL和MySQL上分别达到80.1%和82.3%的覆盖率，并适用于闭源系统KingbaseES。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDBMS测试中实现高覆盖率面临挑战，传统模糊测试无法捕捉SQL输入与内部执行路径的对应关系，而符号执行存在高昂成本和扩展性限制，需要新的测试方法。

Method: DBcover通过轻量级动态分析提取SQL到路径的对应关系和调用图作为全局上下文，收集目标函数周围的源码信息作为局部上下文，将上下文组织为统一知识图谱用于检索复用，然后进行两阶段测试生成：先选择与未覆盖目标执行路径接近的语义相关种子，再用全局和局部上下文引导LLM生成SQL测试用例。

Result: 在PostgreSQL上达到80.1%的覆盖率，在MySQL上达到82.3%的覆盖率，并且在企业级RDBMS KingbaseES上也表现有效，证明了对闭源系统的实际适用性。

Conclusion: DBcover成功结合LLM的语义理解能力与白盒动态分析的上下文信息，形成了一种可扩展且有效的SQL测试生成方案，不仅适用于开源RDBMS也能应用于闭源系统。

Abstract: Relational Database Management Systems (RDBMSs) are the backbone of modern data-intensive applications, making reliability and robustness critical. However, achieving high coverage in RDBMS testing remains challenging because of large codebases and complex execution logic. Traditional fuzzing relies on random SQL generation and cannot capture the correspondence between SQL inputs and internal execution paths, while symbolic execution suffers from prohibitive cost and scalability limitations.
  We propose DBcover, an LLM-driven white-box SQL test generation framework based on contextual reasoning. DBcover uses lightweight dynamic analysis to extract SQL-to-path correspondence and call graphs as global context, and collects source-level information around target functions as local context. These contexts are organized in a unified knowledge graph for efficient retrieval and reuse. DBcover then performs two-phase test generation: it first selects a semantically relevant seed whose execution path is close to the uncovered target, and then guides the LLM with global and local context to generate SQL test cases that trigger previously uncovered code regions. Experiments show that DBcover achieves 80.1% and 82.3% coverage on PostgreSQL and MySQL, and is also effective on the enterprise RDBMS KingbaseES, demonstrating its practical applicability to closed-source systems.

</details>


### [5] [PolyMemDB: A Polyglot Database System for AI Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25577)
*Yu Wang,Jiaheng Lu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PolyMemDB是一个专为智能代理记忆管理设计的新型多语言存储系统，通过多范式存储架构和概率推理引擎解决现有记忆系统数据碎片化和缺乏细粒度溯源导致的事实冲突与幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理记忆系统存在两大局限：一是依赖单一存储范式导致多维数据碎片化；二是缺乏细粒度数据溯源能力，无法解决长期事实冲突，加剧了LLM幻觉问题。

Method: PolyMemDB采用多语言（polyglot）存储架构，支持图、向量、概率和时空数据等多种记忆类型的统一管理；并集成了概率推理引擎，结合时间衰减与半环聚合（semiring aggregation），实现长期事实冲突的消解和推理链的可追溯。

Result: 系统能够有效解决长期事实冲突，提供详细的数据溯源，并支持用户透明地追踪推理链，从而减少LLM幻觉并增强个性化体验。

Conclusion: PolyMemDB为智能代理记忆管理提供了统一的多范式存储与概率推理方案，是解决大规模异构代理数据管理问题的有效途径。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of personal intelligent agents, users generate massive, heterogeneous data during long-term interactions. Leveraging this data as long-term memory helps reduce token overhead and deliver personalized experiences. However, existing memory systems face two primary limitations: they rely on single-storage paradigms that fragment multi-dimensional data, and they lack fine-grained data provenance to resolve long-term factual conflicts, thereby worsening LLM hallucinations.
  In this demonstration, we introduce PolyMemDB, a novel system tailored for managing agent memory. PolyMemDB has a polyglot storage architecture designed to track and manage various memory types, including graph, vector, probability and spatial-temporal data. To ensure factual consistency and reduce hallucinations, it features a probabilistic inference engine that integrates temporal decay with semiring aggregation, resolving long-term factual conflicts, providing detailed data provenance, and enabling users to trace reasoning chains transparently.

</details>


### [6] [MetaSieve: Faster Relational Deep Learning through SQL-Based Metapath Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25903)
*Fahim Shahriar Khan,Ashraf Aboulnaga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaSieve，一种基于元路径选择的层，通过利用关系数据库的连接和聚合能力，在保持甚至提升精度的前提下大幅减少关系深度学习（RDL）中GNN的训练子图大小，从而显著降低训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系深度学习（RDL）将多表关系数据库建模为图并用GNN训练，但训练成本主要由每个种子节点周围的子图大小决定。现有方法没有考虑子图中许多元路径可以被剪枝而不损失精度，导致训练效率低下。

Method: MetaSieve通过SQL连接和聚合查询计算每个候选元路径扩展的统计信息，基于新颖的评分函数评估扩展的信息量和轻量性，将得分低于阈值的元路径视为无信息并剪枝。该选择过程仅依赖数据库统计和任务标签，与GNN参数无关，因此可适配多种GNN架构。

Result: 在RelBench基准测试上，使用多种GNN骨干网络的实验表明，MetaSieve一致地大幅减少每轮训练时间，同时保持甚至经常提升预测精度。

Conclusion: MetaSieve证明了在RDL中，通过数据库能力进行元路径剪枝是一种轻量且有效的方法，能够在不牺牲精度的情况下显著提升训练效率，并具有跨GNN架构的通用性。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) is an effective approach to machine learning over multi-table relational databases. In RDL, a database is modeled as a graph in which each row is a node and each foreign-key relation is an edge, and a graph neural network (GNN) is trained on this graph. Training a GNN requires sampling a subgraph around every seed node in the training set, and the cost of training is largely determined by the size of these subgraphs. This paper aims to reduce subgraph size by leveraging the join and aggregation capabilities of relational database systems. We observe that sampled subgraphs are obtained by following metapaths composed of foreign-key links, and that many of these metapaths can be pruned without loss of accuracy. We present MetaSieve, a metapath selection layer that determines which metapaths to retain and which to prune. For each candidate metapath extension, MetaSieve computes statistics via SQL join and aggregation queries and evaluates the extension based on a novel scoring function that prefers lightweight but informative candidates. Metapaths whose scores fall below a threshold are deemed uninformative and pruned. Metapath selection in MetaSieve is lightweight since it relies only on database statistics and task labels, and it is independent of GNN parameters, so it integrates with diverse GNN architectures for classification and regression. Our evaluation on the RelBench benchmark with multiple GNN backbones shows that MetaSieve consistently reduces per-epoch training time by large margins while maintaining and often improving accuracy.

</details>

### [7] [Secret MCP: Evidence-Bounded and Context-Isolated Design Specification Generation from Web Screenshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24944)
*Yeongjin Jo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Secret MCP，一个开源本地系统，从公开网页参考中生成可审计的设计规格说明，通过分离检索、证据准备、模型调用、存储和检查环节，确保每个参考独立处理且证据可追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的截图转代码系统仅优化渲染实现，但截图缺乏文档结构、交互逻辑、响应式规则和来源信息，无法区分观察结果与猜测。多参考提示词还会导致参考之间的证据或推断污染。

Method: 系统采用模块化架构分离各处理环节。长截图被缩放并带重叠平铺；证据记录保留源空间和准备空间的坐标及测量调色板。使用包含19个章节的契约覆盖页面清单、导航几何、响应式矩阵、组件、可访问性、验收标准等，并对测量、观察、推断和未知声明进行显式标记。参考按顺序通过采样器接口处理，MCP适配器为每个参考发送单独的sampling请求。

Result: 在实时检索和固定模型集成测试中，排除了一个参考后选择两个参考，准备了9张证据图像，发出2次采样请求，生成2个文档且零交叉引用标识符。静态审计3个外部生成的设计索引发现每个文档包含全部19个必需章节、每文档一个唯一参考标识符、8个页面规格、1,943个像素值测量和319个颜色字面量。

Conclusion: 测试验证了编排不变量和语法契约合规性，而非语义或视觉重建准确性。采样器抽象在MCP采样于2026年弃用后仍能在直接模型API和传输层间保持这些边界。

Abstract: Screenshot-to-code systems optimize for rendered implementations, but screenshots omit document structure, interaction logic, responsive rules, and provenance needed to distinguish observation from guesswork. Multi-reference prompts also risk contaminating one reference with evidence or inferences from another. We present Secret MCP, an open-source local system that produces one auditable design specification per public web reference. It separates retrieval, evidence preparation, model invocation, storage, and inspection. Long captures are resized and tiled with overlap; evidence records preserve prepared- and source-space coordinates and a measured color palette. A 19-section contract covers page inventories, navigation geometry, responsive matrices, components, accessibility, acceptance criteria, and explicit labels for measured, observed, inferred, and unknown claims. References are processed sequentially through a sampler interface. The evaluated MCP adapter sends one sampling/createMessage request per reference with includeContext set to none; a fresh-process adapter provides a stronger boundary.
  We evaluate commit c130c9c at two levels. A live retrieval and fixture-model integration run selected two references after excluding a third, prepared nine evidence images, issued two sampling requests, and produced two documents with zero cross-reference identifier occurrences. A static audit of three externally generated design indexes found all 19 required sections in every document, one unique reference identifier per document, eight page specifications, 1,943 pixel-valued measurements, and 319 color literals. These tests establish orchestration invariants and syntactic contract compliance, not semantic or visual reconstruction accuracy. The sampler abstraction preserves these boundaries across direct model APIs and transports despite MCP sampling's 2026 deprecation.

</details>


### [8] [The Evolution of Binary Decompilation in the Modern Era: A Taxonomy, Literature Review, and Future Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24955)
*Omar Abusabha,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对过去数十年间发表的逆向编译（反编译）研究进行了系统性综述，构建了当代研究方法论的全面分类体系，分析了评估指标、工具和基准的趋势，指出了可靠地面真值缺失和标准化基准不足等关键挑战，并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反编译已成为软件工程和安全分析中的基础技术，且正通过机器学习的融合不断演进。然而，该领域缺乏系统性的综述来梳理方法论、评估标准和研究趋势，导致不同方法之间难以进行严格比较。

Method: 作者对过去数十年发表的反编译研究进行了系统性文献回顾，开发了一个涵盖当代研究方法论的全面分类学（taxonomy），并分析了评估指标、工具和基准的使用趋势。

Result: 综述揭示了领域内存在的关键挑战，包括可靠地面真值（ground truth）的缺乏以及标准化基准的缺失，这些问题阻碍了不同方法之间的严格比较。

Conclusion: 作者指出了领域内的重要短板，并提出了未来研究方向的建议，以推动反编译领域更系统化的发展。

Abstract: Decompilation has become a foundational technique in software engineering and security analysis, and it is now advancing through the integration of modern machine learning (ML) approaches. This article presents a systematic review of decompilation studies published over the past decades and develops a comprehensive taxonomy of methodologies employed in contemporary research. We further examine trends in evaluation metrics, tools, and benchmarks used to assess state-of-the-art approaches. Our review reveals key challenges, such as the lack of reliable ground truth and the absence of standardized benchmarks, which hinder rigorous comparison. Finally, we outline future research directions.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating and Preventing Security Smells in AI-Generated Ansible Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24962)
*Pandu Ranga Reddy Konala,Vimal Kumar,David Bainbridge,Junaid Haseeb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次评估了16个AI编程助手生成基础设施即代码（Ansible角色）的安全性，发现所有模型在无安全指导下生成的代码均存在安全缺陷，无法通过CIS合规验证。作者提出基于扩展CO-STAR框架的提示词方法，将Ansible最佳实践和CIS基准整合到提示中，使4个模型能生成合规代码，最佳模型达到95%-100%的CIS符合率，是人类开发者（23%-43%）的四倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码助手能生成基础设施即代码，但此前没有任何研究检验这些代码是否满足安全要求。由于基础设施代码中的安全缺陷会传播到部署系统，导致不安全且不可信的基础设施，因此亟需评估并解决这一问题。

Method: 评估16个AI模型生成Apache Tomcat v10和MongoDB v7的Ansible角色，分析278个角色对照CIS基准。提出扩展CO-STAR框架，将Ansible最佳实践与CIS基准整合到提示词中，在代码合成阶段预防安全缺陷，而非部署后检测。

Result: 无安全指导时，所有16个模型生成的代码均含安全缺陷。应用提出的方法后，16个模型中有4个能生成合规代码，领先模型达到95%-100%的CIS合规率，相比人类开发者的23%-43%实现四倍改进，整体代码质量提升19%-49%。其余12个模型失败原因在于无法遵循多重约束指令而非无法生成代码。

Conclusion: 对于能力强的模型，该安全增强方法无需重新训练，仅需通过系统提示词即可采用，是一种低成本高效提升AI生成基础设施代码安全性的解决方案。

Abstract: AI coding assistants generate Infrastructure as Code, yet no work has examined whether this code meets security requirements. This matters because security smells in infrastructure code propagate to deployed systems, producing infrastructure that is insecure and untrustworthy. We evaluate 16 AI models generating Ansible roles for Apache Tomcat v10 and MongoDB v7, analysing 278 Ansible roles against CIS benchmarks. Without security guidance, all 16 AI models produced code containing security smells, resulting in vulnerable infrastructure that fails compliance verification and underperforms code written by human developers. We introduce an approach integrating Ansible best practices and CIS benchmarks into prompts through an extended CO-STAR framework, enabling security smell prevention during synthesis rather than detection after deployment. When this approach is applied, 4 out of 16 models generate compliant code, with the leading model achieving 95%-100% CIS compliance, a fourfold improvement over humans at 23%-43%, with overall code quality improving by 19%-49%. The remaining 12 models fail not because they cannot generate code but because they cannot follow instructions with multiple constraints. For capable models, the approach requires no retraining and can be adopted through system prompts.

</details>


### [10] [ARISMA: Guidelines for AI- and LLM-Assisted Systematic Reviews, Scoping Reviews, and Mapping Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25050)
*Mahyar Tourchi Moghaddam,Mina Alipour*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ARISMA标准，一个用于系统综述中AI辅助报告与整合的端到端操作标准。ARISMA将AI定位为可检查、可基准测试、可记录和可逆的辅助工具，而非自主审稿人，核心原则是所有关键科学决策必须保持人类可解释、可审计和可问责，并提供了生命周期分类、流程指南、治理模型、检查清单和验证矩阵等工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统综述等工作量日益增大，AI/LLM进入综述实践的各个环节，但现有实证证据不均衡且任务依赖性强，不足以支持无约束的自动化。现有标准（PRISMA 2020、PRISMA-S等）虽重要，但均未提供端到端操作标准来规范AI使用的适用性、验证方式、人类主导决策环节以及AI参与的报告方式。

Method: 提出ARISMA框架，涵盖生命周期分类、流程指南、综述管线各步骤的分步建议、治理与溯源模型、工具支持框架、AI整合报告检查清单和验证矩阵；同时处理法律、隐私、基础设施和可持续性考量；框架通过结构化专家咨询进行迭代完善。

Result: 产出ARISMA标准，包括七个核心组成部分（生命周期分类、流程指南、步骤建议、治理模型、工具框架、报告检查清单、验证矩阵），并经专家咨询迭代优化，形成实用且可审计的负责任AI辅助证据综合指南。

Conclusion: ARISMA提供了一个实用、可审计的指南来规范AI辅助证据综合，确保AI作为辅助角色而非自主角色，所有关键科学决策保持人类主导、可问责，填补了现有标准在AI应用规范方面的空白。

Abstract: Systematic reviews, scoping reviews, mapping studies, and related evidence syntheses are increasingly difficult to conduct with fully manual workflows as search volumes, update cycles, and synthesis requirements continue to expand. At the same time, artificial intelligence, machine learning, and large language models are rapidly entering review practice across query formulation, screening, extraction, categorization, appraisal support, and reporting. Yet the empirical evidence remains uneven, task-dependent, and insufficient to justify unconstrained automation. Existing standards such as PRISMA 2020, PRISMA-S, PRISMA-ScR, PRISMA-P, PRESS, and SWiM remain essential, but none provides an end-to-end operational standard for when AI use is methodologically appropriate, how it should be validated, which review decisions must remain human-led, and how AI involvement should be reported so that readers can audit it. This paper proposes ARISMA, an AI Reporting and Integration standard for Systematic Methods and Analysis. ARISMA treats AI as an inspected, benchmarked, logged, and reversible assistant rather than an autonomous reviewer. It is built around one governing principle: every consequential scientific decision must remain human-interpretable, human-auditable, and human-accountable. The paper contributes a lifecycle taxonomy, process guidance, stepwise recommendations across the review pipeline, a governance and provenance model, a tool-support framework, an AI-integrated reporting checklist, and a validation matrix. It also addresses legal, privacy, infrastructure, and sustainability considerations. The framework was iteratively refined through structured expert consultation. The result is a practical and auditable guideline for responsible AI-assisted evidence synthesis.

</details>


### [11] [Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25174)
*James C. Davis,Kelechi Kalu,Huiyun Peng,Parth V. Patil*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出模型驱动的智能体软件工程（MAGE），一个将工程意图、系统结构和验收证据外部化的框架与理论，通过约束、传感器、验证器和门控赋予已定决心愿相应的权威，从而将商品化智能转化为可信赖的自主工程能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体虽能提升实现能力，但不会自动使项目意图、系统结构或验收证据变得明确。随着实现相对于工程判断变得充裕，稀缺工作转向选择有用的抽象、产出证据以及确定哪些义务支配验收。现有工作流（更大提示、仓库检索、逐变更审查）只部分解决了这一缺口，仍需智能体和工程师重建关键属性。

Method: MAGE解决了两个问题：表征问题和权威问题。它外部化回答问题所需的最小有目的的表征，然后通过约束、传感器、验证器和门控，为已定决心愿赋予相称的权威。它保持不确定的意图开放，并将重复的重建和判断转化为可持续继承的工程结构。该方法基于一个纵向案例开发，并通过六个独立报告的工业案例进行完善。

Result: 跨多个来源，MAGE解释了外部化知识、受限行动、独立评估和保留人类权威如何组合成受治理的工程环境，并提出了检验何时该环境能将商品化智能转化为持久工程进步的测试。

Conclusion: MAGE提供了一种系统性替代方案，将编码智能体时代稀缺的工程判断工作制度化，使智能体能力增长的同时保持人类对工程治理的权威。

Abstract: Coding agents increase implementation capacity without automatically making project intent, system structure, or acceptance evidence explicit. As implementation becomes abundant relative to engineering judgment, the scarce work shifts toward choosing useful abstractions, producing evidence, and determining which obligations govern acceptance. Existing workflows address parts of this gap through larger prompts, repository retrieval, or perchange review, but still require agents and engineers to reconstruct consequential properties.
  As an alternative, we present Model-Based Agentic Software Engineering (MAGE). MAGE is a framework and a theory for building trustworthy autonomy from commodity intelligence. MAGE addresses a representation problem and an authority problem: it externalizes the smallest purposeful representation needed to answer an engineering question, then gives settled obligations proportionate authority through constraints, sensors, validators, and gates. It keeps uncertain intent open and turns recurring reconstruction and judgment into durable engineering structure that later work can inherit. We developed MAGE from a longitudinal case and refined it through six independently reported industrial accounts. Across these sources, MAGE explains how externalized knowledge, bounded action, independent evaluation, and retained human authority can compose into a governed engineering environment, and proposes tests of when that environment turns commodity intelligence into durable engineering progress.

</details>


### [12] [SPECMINE: A Large-Scale Corpus of Spec-Driven Development Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25202)
*Shyam Agarwal,Bogdan Vasilescu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SPECMINE是一个大规模语料库，通过两次普查捕获了GitHub公开仓库中的规格驱动开发（SDD）实践：一次覆盖47万+规格文件（含17种工具），另一次针对Kiro工具的特定布局（9.8万+文件）。语料库还包含与规格相关的PR变更集和240万+类型化引用链接，使社区首次能够研究AI时代软件规格化的实际运作方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自2025年以来，规格驱动开发（SDD）工具大量涌现，但这些工具产生的规格工件从未在大规模范围内被研究过。规格如何转化为代码这一核心问题仍悬而未决。

Method: 构建SPECMINE语料库，通过两次普查（广义规格文件普查和Kiro特定布局普查）收集GitHub公开仓库中的规格文件，每条规格附带仓库元数据、完整提交历史和文档结构解析结果；同时针对11种工具扫描涉及规格的PR及其变更集，并建立规格到代码的类型化引用索引。

Result: 收集了470,795个规格文件（73,030个仓库，涉及17种命名工具）和98,574个Kiro格式文件（12,910个仓库），捕获5,992个规格相关PR（581个仓库），索引2,421,323个类型化引用（128万指向代码文件、86.3万指向兄弟文档等）。

Conclusion: SPECMINE为社区提供了首个大规模研究AI智能体时代软件规格化实践的语料库，使最简工作流（规格与实现在同一PR中共同变更）可直接观测，并通过引用索引提供第二条独立的规格到代码链接路径。

Abstract: Spec-Driven Development (SDD) is a fast-emerging practice in which a structured natural-language specification, written by a developer, or (more often) drafted by an AI tool and then curated by the developer, drives an AI coding agent's implementation. A wave of tooling (GitHub Spec Kit [3], OpenSpec [4], AWS Kiro [5], and dozens of others) has appeared since 2025, yet the artifacts these tools produce have never been studied at scale. We present SPECMINE, a corpus that captures SDD in public GitHub repositories through two censuses: a broad census of spec.md/specs.md files covering most tools (470,795 files across 73,030 repositories, attributed to 17 named tools), and a Kiro census of its distinct requirements/design/tasks layout (98,574 files across 12,910 repositories). Each spec is enriched with full repository metadata, complete commit history, and parsed document structure. How a spec becomes code is itself an open question, so for 11 tools we sweep every pull request that touches a spec in their repositories with at least ten stars, capturing 5,992 such PRs across 581 repositories with their changesets. That makes the simplest workflow, spec and implementation changing together in one PR, directly observable, and a census-wide index of 2,421,323 typed references (1.28M to code files, 863k to sibling documents, 152k to PRs, 62k refs, 43k branches, 22k issues) gives a second, independent link from spec to code. SPECMINE lets the community study, for the first time, how software is specified in the age of AI agents.

</details>


### [13] [A Few Pages of Markdown: Committed AI Configuration and Lower Quality Cost after Coding-Agent Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25241)
*Yegor Denisov-Blanch,Shyam Agarwal,Pavel Azaletskiy,Hao He,Rylan Schaeffer,Brando Miranda,Bogdan Vasilescu,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RAMP（Repository AI Maturity Profile），一个四层累积成熟度模型，用于衡量团队在版本控制中提交的AI工具配置。基于441个仓库的实证研究发现，AI代理在不同成熟度层级下均能加速开发（提交量增加28-38%），但代码质量影响存在显著差异：缺乏AI配置的agent-first仓库认知复杂度增幅约为有配置仓库的两倍（+53% vs +27%），静态分析警告增幅为1.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于编码代理的研究仅报告采用者间的平均效应，掩盖了团队间的巨大差异。缺乏一个基于版本控制工件来衡量团队如何配置AI工具的成熟度模型，无法解释为何不同团队在使用AI编码代理时结果差异显著。

Method: 提出RAMP四层累积成熟度模型（从行为规则和编码标准、命名代理定义到多代理编排）；在441个仓库上进行实证验证，检验层级累积性并通过独立人工标注验证标签可靠性（97%一致）；在每层内重新估计已有的代理采用面板模型，对比不同成熟度下的开发速度与代码质量指标（认知复杂度、静态分析警告）。

Result: RAMP各层级表现为累积量表，人工标注准确率达97%；73.8%的AI配置工件提交后从未修改（set-and-forget模式）；在各成熟度层级内，AI代理平均增加28-38%的提交量；但在agent-first仓库中，无AI配置的团队认知复杂度增幅为+53%（有配置为+27%），静态分析警告增幅为1.7倍。

Conclusion: AI编码代理虽能普遍加速开发，但缺乏成熟AI配置的团队面临更严重的代码质量恶化。由于成熟度是观测性变量，工程纪律或模型能力等混杂因素可能部分解释该差距，因此将发现定位为假设生成性质，并公开发布RAMP作为可复用研究工具。

Abstract: Coding agents increase development velocity but also technical debt. Prior work reports only average effects across adopters, hiding wide differences between teams. We introduce RAMP (Repository AI Maturity Profile), a four-level cumulative maturity model grounded in version-controlled artifacts that teams commit to configure AI tools. RAMP runs from behavioral rules and coding standards through named agent definitions to multi-agent orchestration, with observed practice concentrated in the first three levels. Across 441 repositories the levels behave as a cumulative scale, and independent human annotation reproduces RAMP's repository-level labels on 97% of a held-out sample. Adoption is cumulative, forward-only, and set-and-forget: 73.8% of artifacts are committed once and never modified. Re-estimating an existing agent-adoption panel within each stratum, agents accelerate development regardless of maturity (28-38% more commits), but quality diverges: among agent-first repositories, where the contrast is identified, those without committed AI configuration show roughly twice the increase in cognitive complexity (+53% versus +27%) and 1.7x the increase in static-analysis warnings. Because maturity is observational, correlated engineering discipline or model capability may explain part of the gap; we present these findings as hypothesis-generating and release RAMP as a reusable instrument.

</details>


### [14] [Metis: Typed Runtime Mediation for Tool-Using Software Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25322)
*Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Metis，一个多提供商运行时，将提供商流转换为类型化事件后再到达外部效果，通过显式权限决策、干扰类、终端结果和生命周期转换来管理软件代理操作。在30对匹配的真实I/O测试中，四类调解将中位延迟从25.958ms降至14.146ms，平均配对差异为-12.295ms。故障矩阵、子边界消融和权限预言机测试均验证了其调度、权限路由和子权限控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理将概率模型输出连接到实际系统操作（如仓库、进程、网络和图形应用变更），这需要一种运行时机制来在外部效果发生前显式管理和检查权限决策、干扰分类和生命周期状态，确保代理操作的安全性、可审查性和可调试性。

Method: Metis采用多提供商运行时架构：将提供商流转换为类型化事件，在进入外部效果前执行权限判定。执行路径显式化权限决策（四类调解）、干扰类、终端结果和生命周期转换。通过30对匹配真实I/O实验、十案例故障矩阵、子边界消融测试及五条调用路线的权限预言机对比进行评估。

Result: 四类调解使中位延迟从强制串行化的25.958ms降至14.146ms，平均配对差异为-12.295ms（95%引导区间[-12.968,-11.694]），所有配对均更快。十案例故障矩阵暴露了重复标识符和回滚限制；完整的门控加注册条件阻止了未授权效果并隐藏了全部五个逃逸工具；决策权限预言机在五条调用路线上匹配全部十个声明案例；五种模型条件每次3/3完成固定读取标记协议。

Conclusion: 结果表明Metis在调度、权限路由、子授权和提供商验证追踪闭合方面支持有界声明，但作者明确指出该研究不能证明模型能力、语义安全性、回滚能力或优于其他运行时。

Abstract: Software agents connect probabilistic model output to operations that change repositories, processes, networks, and graphical applications. We present Metis, a multi-provider runtime that converts provider streams into typed events before admitted calls reach external effects. Its execution path makes permission decisions, interference classes, terminal results, and lifecycle transitions explicit and inspectable. We evaluate these mechanisms on frozen source artifacts. Across 30 matched real-I/O pairs, four-class mediation reduced median elapsed time from 25.958 ms under forced serialization to 14.146 ms. The mean paired difference was -12.295 ms (95% bootstrap interval [-12.968, -11.694]), with mediation faster in all pairs. A ten-case fault matrix exposed duplicate-identifier and rollback limits. In a child-boundary ablation, the full gate-plus-registry condition blocked the declared unauthorized effect and hid all five escape tools. Removing both protections reversed both observations. A decision-only permission oracle matched all ten declared cases across five invocation routes. Five model conditions also completed a fixed Read-marker protocol in 3/3 trials each. These results support bounded claims about dispatch, permission routing, child authority, and provider-valid trace closure. They do not establish model competence, semantic safety, rollback, or superiority over another runtime.

</details>


### [15] [Point-in-Time Audit Before Alpha: Public-Archive Availability and a Negative Matched-Budget Study on BTC Perpetual Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25348)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang,Peilin Han,Kangnan He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对币安BTCUSDT USD-M永续合约的公开加密数据进行了严格审计，发现初始数据存在长达304.57天的缺口，修订后仅保留727个完整UTC日。审计工具识别出40/40的违规模板且无误拒合法模板，将空信号的误通过率从0.2910降至0.0625。在历史留出集上，所有评估策略虽有正IC但净夏普为负，因此报告了一个有界负面结果而非盈利或优越性声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开加密货币档案可能看似可用，但因子研究要求每个决策时刻都有可观测、可执行的数据。作者旨在严格审计公开币安永续合约数据的事件时间、发布时间和可用性时间，以评估其真实可靠性。

Method: 采用事件时间、发布时间和可用性时间三重时间戳进行审计，区分确定性与审计/评估/留出访问。设定无缺口五分钟数据要求，对交易、标记、指数和未平仓量进行检验；修订后以交易、标记、指数和已实现资金费率为核心流。构建规则模板审计器对80个冻结模板进行测试，并比较全审计与部分审计的空信号误通过率。

Result: 初始无缺口要求失败，最长未修复缺口达304.57天。修订后的数据保留727个完整UTC天，采用436/145/146天的训练/验证/历史留出划分。审计器检测出40/40违规且零误拒合法模板。全审计将平均误通过率从0.2910降至0.0625。审计后的自适应代理与随机搜索持平，未建立优越性。历史留出集上所有策略IC为正但净夏普为负。

Conclusion: 作者报告一个有界负面结果：在透明严格的审计条件下，公开的币安永续合约数据无法支持盈利性或代理优越性声明。数据完整性和可执行性是因子研究的核心前提，审计后的可靠数据范围远小于表面可用范围。

Abstract: Public cryptocurrency archives may appear usable when files exist, although factor research requires observations available and executable at each decision time. We audit public Binance BTCUSDT USD-M perpetual-futures data using event, publication, and availability times and separate proposal from deterministic auditing, evaluation, and holdout access. An initial gapless five-minute requirement for trade, mark, index, and open interest failed: the longest unrepaired intersection was 304.5729166666667 days. A disclosed revision made trade, mark, index, and realized funding the core streams and made open interest optional because its publication time was unverified. The revised mask retained 727 complete UTC days and supported a 436/145/146-day train, validation, and historical-holdout split. On 80 frozen known-rule templates, the auditor detected 40/40 violations and rejected 0/40 legal templates. Across ten null-signal paths, full auditing reduced mean false passes from 0.2910 to 0.0625. Under matched valid-candidate budgets, the audited adaptive agent tied random search and did not establish superiority. In the one-time historical holdout, all evaluated runs had positive IC but negative net Sharpe under primary costs. We therefore report a scoped negative result rather than a profitability or agent-superiority claim.

</details>


### [16] [Retry Amplification in Distributed Systems: A Systematic Analysis of Retry Policies and Their Role in Cascading Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25403)
*Rishabh Mehan,Jasmit Kaur Saluja*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式系统中重试机制的集体行为，提出了重试放大因子（RAF）指标，通过对200个开源Python微服务项目的分析发现重试逻辑的普遍性及其反模式，并提出自适应重试预算（ARB）方法来改善系统级重试行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重试机制是分布式系统韧性的标准组件，但每个调用层的重试策略是独立配置的，缺乏对重试机制整体集体行为的理解。在部分故障期间，各层同时重试可能产生放大的请求流量，导致成功率和系统韧性反而下降。

Method: 引入重试放大因子（RAF）量化重试策略在部分故障期间产生的额外请求量；分析200个开源Python微服务项目中的重试逻辑；通过模拟实验（每策略100次试验）评估不同重试策略在相关故障下的表现；提出自适应重试预算（ARB）机制。

Result: 200个项目中11.5%检测到显式重试逻辑，实际流行率约41%；60.9%的项目存在无退避配置；113个生产配置中仅1个随机化延迟。在相关故障下，天真的标准重试策略将成功率从55.4%降至41.5%（差于不重试）。预算约束的重试可维持接近无重试基线的成功率同时恢复瞬时故障。

Conclusion: 重试行为应被设计为系统级属性，而非在每个调用点局部配置。自适应重试预算（ARB）是有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Retry mechanisms are a standard component of resilient distributed systems, but their collective behavior, when every tier in a call path retries concurrently, is less well understood than the per-client guidance that produced them. This paper introduces the retry amplification factor (RAF), a metric quantifying the additional request volume that retry policies generate during partial failures. In a study of 200 open-source Python microservice projects, explicit retry logic is detected in 11.5%, and an audit of our own false negatives places true prevalence near 41%. Among the projects detected, 60.9% contain at least one configuration without backoff, and after manual verification exactly one of 113 production configurations randomizes its delay. We then evaluate these policies in simulation (n = 100 trials per strategy). Under correlated failures, a naive standard retry policy reduces the success rate from 55.4% to 41.5% relative to performing no retries at all. We catalog five recurring anti-patterns, propose Adaptive Retry Budgeting (ARB), and show that budget-constrained retries maintain success rates close to the no-retry baseline while still recovering from transient faults. These results indicate that retry behavior should be designed as a system-level property rather than configured locally at each call site.

</details>


### [17] [RotDroid: Cross-Orientation State Equivalence Testing for Detecting GUI Rotation Bugs in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25425)
*Mengdi Qin,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RotDroid是一个用于检测Android应用屏幕旋转非崩溃功能缺陷（NCFs）的测试框架，通过跨方向的GUI状态等价性检查来发现旋转导致的布局不一致和状态丢失等问题，并已发现94个未知缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕旋转是Android应用中的基础交互，但经常引入难以自动检测的非崩溃功能缺陷（如布局不一致和状态丢失），其核心挑战在于缺乏有效的测试预言（test oracle）来验证横竖屏之间的状态等价性。

Method: RotDroid包含三个关键组件：1) 通过生成和变异状态保持动作序列（SPS）来构造跨方向的语义等价GUI状态；2) 构建RotBench数据集，包含配对的横竖屏GUI状态对；3) 开发RotVL，一个针对等价性检查进行微调的视觉语言模型作为可靠预言。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，RotVL优于现有的最先进模型；在同等预算下，RotDroid比现有技术检测出更多的旋转诱导缺陷；在大型开源和闭源应用研究中，RotDroid报告了94个先前未知的缺陷，其中47个已被开发者确认或修复。

Conclusion: RotDroid通过结合状态保持序列生成和视觉语言模型赋能的等价性检查，有效解决了Android屏幕旋转缺陷检测中的测试预言难题，实验证明其在实际应用中具有很强的缺陷发现能力和实用性。

Abstract: Screen rotation is a fundamental interaction in Android applications, but it often introduces non-crashing functional failures (NCFs), such as layout inconsistencies and state loss, which are difficult to detect automatically. A key challenge is the lack of effective test oracles for checking cross-orientation state equivalence between portrait and landscape views. We propose RotDroid, a testing framework for detecting GUI rotation bugs via cross-orientation state equivalence. RotDroid generates and mutates State-Preserving action Sequences (SPS) to construct semantically equivalent GUI states across orientations. To support reliable oracle checking, we build RotBench, a dataset of paired portrait-landscape GUI states, and develop RotVL, a vision-language model fine-tuned for equivalence checking. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that RotVL outperforms state-of-the-art models, and RotDroid detects more rotation-induced failures than existing techniques under equal budgets. In large-scale studies on open- and closed-source apps, RotDroid reports 94 previously unknown bugs, with 47 confirmed or fixed by developers, demonstrating its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [18] [A Hybrid Usability Approach for Rating Evaluation of M-Commerce Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25550)
*Ahmad Ibtisam,Bilal Khan,Arshad Ali*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合可用性模型来预测移动商务应用的评分，通过分析8个可用性因素（可学习性、一致性、人为因素、沟通性、有效性、可操作性、效率、满意度）及各因素下的标准，对5个知名M-commerce应用收集40名用户数据进行研究，并使用前向逐步多元线性回归构建评分预测模型，最终通过PRED(x)和K折交叉验证进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用的成功取决于其有用性，而评分是衡量有用性的重要指标。现有研究缺乏针对M-commerce应用可用性因素与评分之间关系的系统探索，因此需要识别影响评分的可用性因素并构建预测模型。

Method: 首先探索现有可用性模型中的因素和标准，基于5个知名M-commerce应用（daraz、shophive、home shopping、Symbios、yayvo）进行评估；然后构建包含8个因素的混合可用性模型，从每个应用的40名用户处收集数据；接着使用前向逐步多元线性回归分析各因素的标准来构建评分预测模型；最后使用PRED(x)和K折技术进行模型评估与验证。

Result: 通过前向逐步多元线性回归分析混合可用性模型中各因素的标准，构建了M-commerce应用评分预测模型，并使用PRED(x)和K折技术对模型进行了评估和验证。

Conclusion: 研究表明混合可用性模型中的各因素及其标准能够有效预测M-commerce应用的评分，所提出的基于回归的预测模型经过验证具有较高的可靠性和有效性。

Abstract: The success of any mobile application relies on its usefulness and rating is considered as an important measure in this regard. This research work focuses on identifying usability factors, which contribute significantly towards the rating of M-commerce apps. This work intends to explore existing usability models consisting of different factors along with a set of criteria and evaluate in terms of rating estimation by considering 5 well-known mobile applications, namely (i) daraz, (ii) shophive, (iii) home shopping, (iv) Symbios. (v) yayvo. Then, this work provides a hybrid usability model for rating prediction of M-commerce applications. The initial hybrid usability model comprises of (i) learnability, (ii) consistency, (iii) human factors,(iv)communicativeness,(v)effectiveness, (vi) Operability, (vii) efficiency, (viii) satisfaction. Each factor consists of some criteria. Keeping in view the factors of hybrid usability model, the data was collected from 40 users for each application. Furthermore, Forward Stepwise Multiple Linear Regression based rating prediction model is suggested by analyzing each criterion of all factors of hybrid usability model. Finally, the model is assessed and validated by using PRED(x) and K-fold techniques.

</details>


### [19] [From General Agents to RCA Experts: A Self-Evolving Harness for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25661)
*Haiyu Huang,Jiewei Lyu,Zhihan Jiang,Jinyang Liu,Xiao He,Tieying Zhang,Wu Xiang,Michael R. Lyu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OpsHarness是一个自进化的根因分析（RCA）外部适配层框架，通过复用量产通用智能体的能力，将诊断经验转化为可复用的专业知识，在保持高准确率的同时实现持续自我进化。实验表明其top-1准确率达到59.0%，相比裸通用智能体提升63.4%，相比基线RCA智能体提升4.02倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的RCA自动化有两种路径：直接使用通用智能体（如Codex、Claude Code）或从头构建专用RCA智能体。定量研究发现前者表现已超过后者，但准确率仍不满足生产需求，瓶颈主要在于智能体外部的适配层（harness）。因此，研究认为应聚焦于外部适配层的构建，而不是重新从零开发智能体。

Method: OpsHarness包含数据平面和控制平面两个核心部分。数据平面集成分层运维知识与idea-card工具库；控制平面负责协调设置、诊断、进化和验证四个环节。进化过程中，系统对比成功与失败的诊断轨迹，将其证据转化为原子化提案，并通过双重门控验证机制防止过拟合和回归，从而实现经验积累和自我演进。

Result: 在两个公开基准测试和一个工业部署中，OpsHarness达到59.0%的top-1准确率，比裸通用智能体提升63.4%，比基线RCA智能体提升4.02倍。结果验证了"聚焦外部适配层而非重建智能体"这一核心论点的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文主张LLM驱动的RCA应聚焦于自进化的外部适配层设计，通过复用现代通用智能体的强大通用能力，将诊断经验持续转化为可复用专业知识，从而在准确率和实用性上超越从头构建的专用RCA智能体方案。

Abstract: Automated root cause analysis (RCA) with large language models (LLMs) has drawn growing attention. Today, SREs typically automate RCA with LLMs in one of two ways: directly using a general-purpose agent (e.g., Codex or Claude Code) for diagnosis, or building a specialized RCA agent from scratch. As mainstream general agents grow more capable and iterate quickly, our quantitative study finds that the former now often surpasses the latter. Its accuracy, however, still falls short of production needs, and this gap stems mainly from the external adaptation layer outside the agent's general capabilities, namely the harness. We therefore argue that LLM-based RCA should focus on this external harness, reusing the strong general capabilities of a modern agent rather than rebuilding an agent from scratch. A key capability of such a harness is to self-evolve, accumulating system-specific experience from past diagnoses so that it gets better the more it is used. We introduce OpsHarness, a self-evolving RCA harness that turns diagnosis experience into reusable expertise. Its data plane combines layered operational knowledge with an idea-card tool library, while its control plane coordinates setup, diagnosis, evolution, and verification. During evolution, OpsHarness contrasts successful and failed trajectories, converts their evidence into atomic proposals, and admits updates only through a dual-gate verification process designed to prevent overfitting and regression. Across two public benchmarks and an industrial deployment, OpsHarness achieves 59.0\% top-1 accuracy, improving over a bare general agent by 63.4\% and over baseline RCA agents by 4.02$\times$.

</details>


### [20] [Predicting Struggling Students in CS1 Programming Using Keystroke-Level Editing Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25769)
*Yasuyo Kofune,Kazumasa Shimari,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在CS1编程练习中，利用键盘级别的日志数据（CodeBench平台）在早期检测陷入困境的学生。研究将学生分为突破型（BT）和完全卡住型（FS）两组，通过对比三种特征配置（仅执行日志、仅CodeMirror编辑日志、两者结合）来回答两个研究问题：键盘级编辑特征是否能提升预测效果，以及在哪个阶段预测最准确。实验基于507名学生的数据，发现最早阶段的预测信号最强，结合特征比仅执行日志提升AUROC 0.098（0.674 vs 0.575），表明键盘级编辑日志能为早期干预提供额外价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一些学生在CS1编程练习中无法在练习结束前达到正确解决方案，而当问题从成绩或最终结果中显现时，教师提供及时干预以帮助学生恢复的机会可能已经错过。因此需要探索在早期阶段就能识别陷入困境学生的方法。

Method: 使用CodeBench平台收集的键盘级实时代码编辑事件日志、执行日志和提交日志。将学生分为突破型（BT，最终提交满分）和完全卡住型（FS，所有提交均为0分）两组，比较三种特征配置（ExecOnly、CMOnly、Combined）在基于提交的逐阶段预测中的表现，使用AUROC作为评估指标。

Result: 在最早阶段，CMOnly优于ExecOnly（AUROC 0.654 vs 0.575），Combined比ExecOnly提升+0.098（AUROC 0.674）。所有配置下，最早阶段产生的预测信号最强。

Conclusion: 练习开始时的行为信号包含关于学生最终能否解决问题的有用线索，键盘级编辑日志为早期优先干预提供了超越执行日志的额外价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the feasibility of early detection of struggling students during CS1 programming exercises using keystroke-level logs. Some students fail to reach a correct solution before the exercise ends, and by the time this becomes apparent from grades or final outcomes, the opportunity for timely instructor support aimed at helping them recover may have passed. We use data from the CodeBench platform, which records real-time code editing events at the keystroke level, alongside execution and submission logs. We define two outcome groups: Breakthrough (BT) students, whose prior submissions all receive 0% and whose final submission achieves full credit, and Fully Stuck (FS) students, whose submissions all receive 0% without reaching a correct solution. To examine this feasibility, we focus on two questions: (RQ1) whether adding keystroke-level editing features improves the prediction of FS students over execution-log features alone, and (RQ2) at which stage BT and FS students can be predicted most accurately. Experiments on the 2019-1 semester of the CodeBench dataset, comprising 507 students, compare three feature configurations: execution-based features (ExecOnly), CodeMirror-based features (CMOnly), and their combination (Combined). We evaluate prediction across successive submission-based stages during each exercise. In the earliest stage, CMOnly outperforms ExecOnly (AUROC 0.654 vs. 0.575), and Combined further improves over ExecOnly by +0.098 (AUROC 0.674). Across all configurations, the earliest stage yielded the strongest predictive signal. These findings indicate that behavioral signals present at the very start of an exercise contain useful clues about whether a student will ultimately solve the problem, and that keystroke-level editing logs provide additional value for early prioritization beyond execution logs alone.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond the Editing Canvas: Evidence Divergence in OOXML-to-LLM Ingestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25880)
*Side Liu,Jiangpeng Liu,Jinwen Xin,Guojun Peng,Jiang Ming*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了OOXML（Word/Excel/PowerPoint）文档在LLM流水线中被提取时可能产生与Microsoft Office界面显示不一致的"证据分叉"问题，即同一份规范有效的OOXML文件在不同消费视角下呈现不同内容。作者系统挖掘出21种证据分叉机制，测试了13种提取工具、4种原生LLM API和7种网页聊天机器人，发现LLM在48-76%的试验中会提取到Office中不可见的内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM流水线越来越多地将OOXML文档作为金融、合规和检索增强生成工作流中的第一手证据，隐含假设模型消费的证据与Microsoft Office编辑画布中显示的内容一致。然而这一假设可能失败：同一份规范有效的OOXML文件在Office中产生一种证据视图，为LLM提取时产生另一种视图，形成"多重真实"（plural ground truth）的不确定状态。

Method: 作者系统遍历和挖掘OOXML规范，在Excel、Word和PowerPoint中确认了21种证据分叉，覆盖视图构建的六个维度。构建了13种工具的提取面板，测试了4种原生LLM输入API和7种网页聊天机器人。每个测试文档都包含一个"陷阱"：在提取内容中出现但在Office中不显示的与任务相关的事实。

Result: 13种提取工具中全部至少从一个分叉机制发出证据。4种API在21种机制的评估中，48-76%的试验返回了陷阱内容；21种机制中有20种至少有一个（共11种接口）返回了陷阱。暴露程度受上游提取路径和提取器配置的影响。对16个热门开源LLM项目的源码调查显示，默认的OOXML摄入路径集中在受影响大的提取器家族上。

Conclusion: OOXML到LLM的流水线中不存在默认的可信证据语义完整性。证据分叉现象普遍存在，模型暴露的内容由摄入路径和提取器配置决定，而非文档自身。这一发现对依赖Office文档作为证据的LLM工作流构成严重安全性和合规性风险。

Abstract: LLM pipelines increasingly ingest Office Open XML (OOXML) documents (Word, Excel, and PowerPoint files) as first-class evidence in financial, compliance, and retrieval-augmented workflows, implicitly assuming semantic integrity: that the evidence consumed by the model matches the content shown in the Microsoft Office suite editing canvas. We show that this assumption can fail in OOXML-to-LLM pipelines. The same specification-valid OOXML file can yield one evidentiary view in Microsoft Office and another when extracted for an LLM. Each view is treated as authoritative by its consumer, a condition we call plural ground truth. The ingestion contract rarely states which view and semantic roles become model evidence or preserves how that evidence was derived. We call the specification-grounded OOXML constructions that induce such divergence evidence forks.
  We systematically traverse and mine the OOXML specification and confirm 21 evidence forks across Excel, Word, and PowerPoint, spanning six dimensions of view construction. All 13 tools in our extraction panel emit evidence from at least one fork. We test four native-ingestion LLM APIs and seven web chatbots. Each test document carries a trap: a task-relevant fact exposed by extraction but not shown in Office. Across this 21-mechanism evaluation, the four APIs return the trap in 48--76% of trials. For 20 of 21 mechanisms, at least one of the eleven interfaces returns the trap. Our measurements further show that exposure is shaped upstream of the model by the ingestion path and extractor configuration. A source-level survey of sixteen popular open-source LLM projects further shows that default OOXML ingestion paths concentrate on affected extractor families.

</details>


### [22] [Answer Is Cheap, Show Me the Evidence! Augmenting Automated Vulnerability Assessment with Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25905)
*Shengyi Pan,Zelong Zheng,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EAVA框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）进行软件漏洞评估，不仅预测评估结果，还提供支持性证据。EAVA通过专门化的LLM代理处理富文本内容和项目信息，采用两阶段训练流程（监督指令微调+强化学习）构建评估模型，并检索历史相似漏洞作为补充证据。在新增数据集上，EAVA相比最强基线的多个指标提升5.3%至35.2%，消融实验和用户研究均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化软件漏洞评估方法往往忽略富文本（如截图、代码片段）和漏洞项目上下文信息，且只关注预测精度而不提供解释或支持证据，限制了分析人员验证不完美预测的实际应用。

Method: 提出EAVA框架：1）使用专门化的LLM代理处理富文本内容和项目信息；2）通过两阶段训练流程构建评估模型——先使用自动标注的推理轨迹进行监督指令微调注入领域知识，再应用强化学习提升内在推理能力；3）检索相似历史漏洞作为补充证据。

Result: 在新的SVR数据集上，EAVA相比最强基线在多个指标上提升5.3%至35.2%；消融实验证实了评估专用模型训练和信息增强的有效性；安全专家用户研究表明EAVA提供的证据对真实场景中的软件漏洞评估实用且有效。

Conclusion: EAVA通过LLM代理、两阶段训练和相似漏洞检索，在提升软件漏洞评估精度的同时提供可解释的证据支持，经实验和用户研究验证，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Software vulnerability (SV) assessment helps prioritize remediation by characterizing reported vulnerabilities. Existing
  automated methods predict assessment results from SV reports (SVRs), but often overlook information in rich text, such as
  screenshots and code snippets, as well as contextual information about vulnerable projects. They also focus on prediction
  accuracy without providing explanations or supporting evidence, limiting their practical use when analysts must validate
  imperfect predictions. We propose EAVA, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to assess SVs and provide
  supporting evidence. EAVA employs specialized LLM agents to process rich-text content and project information, and builds a
  dedicated assessment model through a two-stage training pipeline. It first uses supervised instruction tuning on automatically
  annotated reasoning trajectories to inject domain knowledge, and then applies reinforcement learning to improve intrinsic
  reasoning. EAVA also retrieves similar historical vulnerabilities as supplementary evidence. Experiments on a newly collected
  SVR dataset show that EAVA outperforms the strongest baseline by 5.3 to 35.2 percent across multiple metrics. Ablation studies
  confirm the effectiveness of assessment-specific model training and information enrichment. A user study with security experts
  further demonstrates that the evidence provided by EAVA is useful and practical for real-world SV assessment.

</details>


### [23] [XREPOTEST: Benchmarking Multilingual Repository-Level Unit Test Generation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25939)
*Dung Le Quang,Dong Cao Van,Nam Le Hai,Linh Ngo Van,Anh M. T. Bui,Phuong T. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: XREPOTEST是一个多语言仓库级单元测试生成基准，覆盖Rust、Go、Julia、PHP和Ruby五种语言，通过容器化执行框架和多上下文增强策略，揭示了LLM在独立设置与仓库级设置下的性能差距，并引入Invocation Rate新指标评估测试有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM单元测试生成的评测大多依赖独立设置且编程语言覆盖窄，低估了实际软件环境中的真实难度，缺乏对多语言仓库级场景的系统评估。

Method: 构建覆盖五种语言的仓库级基准，采用容器化执行框架模拟真实仓库约束，设计文件级、LSP和检索三种上下文增强策略，并提出Invocation Rate指标衡量测试是否真正调用目标功能。对14个先进LLM进行系统评测。

Result: 实验表明独立设置与仓库级性能存在显著差距，更丰富的上下文与测试可靠性之间存在权衡；不同LLM在多种语言上的表现存在明显差异。

Conclusion: XREPOTEST为推进真实软件环境中可扩展且稳健的单元测试生成提供了具有挑战性和信息量的基准，数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automated unit test generation, but existing evaluations largely rely on standalone settings and a narrow set of programming languages, overestimating real-world readiness. We introduce XREPOTEST, a multilingual repository-level benchmark for unit test generation spanning five underexplored languages: Rust, Go, Julia, PHP, and Ruby. XREPOTEST evaluates tests under realistic repository constraints using a containerized execution framework and multiple context augmentation strategies, including file-level, LSP-based, and retrieval-based context. Beyond standard metrics such as test pass rate and coverage, we propose Invocation Rate (IR) to assess whether generated tests meaningfully exercise the intended functionality. Experiments with 14 state-of-the-art LLMs, including Claude 4.5, GPT-5.2, DeepSeek V4-Pro, and Qwen families, reveal a substantial gap between standalone and repository-level performance, as well as trade-offs between richer context and test reliability. Overall, XREPOTEST provides a challenging and informative benchmark to advance scalable and robust unit test generation in realistic software environments. The dataset and code are publicly available at: https://github.com/solis-team/XRepoTest

</details>


### [24] [Vulnerable Code Search: Transferable Attack for Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26031)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Jesse Thomason,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨编程语言、可迁移的对抗性攻击方法，通过扰动代码中的标识符（不改变代码功能）来使无关代码片段与目标查询人为对齐，显著降低代码检索系统性能（MRR最多下降77%），并且从小型模型（如CodeT5+）成功迁移到大型闭源模型（如Voyage-code-3、Gemini-3.1-Pro），揭示了当前神经代码语言模型在检索任务中的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码检索对开发者效率至关重要，但现有的神经代码语言模型容易受到针对非功能性文本元素的对抗性攻击，目前缺乏对此类漏洞的系统性研究和有效攻击方法。

Method: 提出一种与编程语言无关、可迁移的对抗性攻击方法，通过扰动代码片段中的标识符（在不改变功能的前提下）人为地将代码与目标查询对齐，利用小型代码嵌入模型（如CodeT5+）计算攻击，再迁移到更大的闭源嵌入模型和LLM上。

Result: 该方法显著提升了查询与无关代码片段之间的相似度，使最先进代码检索模型的MRR（平均倒数排名）最多下降77%，且攻击成功从小型白盒模型迁移到大型闭源模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明当前代码搜索方法存在明显的脆弱性，亟需开发更鲁棒、更具语义感知能力的代码检索方法。

Abstract: Reliable code retrieval is crucial for developer productivity and effective code reuse. However, current neural code language models (CLMs) powering search tools are susceptible to adversarial attacks targeting non-functional textual elements. In this paper, we introduce a programming language-agnostic, transferable, adversarial attack that exploits this CLM vulnerability. Our approach perturbs identifiers within a code snippet without altering the snippet's functionality to artificially align the code with a target query. We demonstrate that our attack, even when computed using smaller code embedding models, such as CodeT5+, is highly effective and transferable to larger, closed-source embedding models, like Voyage-code-3, or LLMs like Gemini-3.1-Pro. Our attack can increase the similarity between the query and arbitrary, irrelevant code snippets, consequently degrading key retrieval metrics such as the Mean Reciprocal Rank (MRR) of state-of-the-art models by up to 77%. The experimental results highlight the fragility of current code search methods and underscore the need for more robust, semantic-aware approaches.

</details>

### [25] [SWIM: Step-Wise Integrated Measure for Session-supervised List Evaluation in Generative Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25104)
*Yuanhao Pu,Chenghao Zhang,Chao Feng,Xunyong Yang,Xiang Li,Yongqi Liu,Defu Lian,Kaiqiao Zhan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出SWIM（Step-Wise Integrated Measure），一种列表级评估器，用于解决工业推荐系统G-E框架中传统评估器忽略会话级动态的问题。SWIM将用户行为建模为有限时域前缀会话级生存过程，通过因果掩码Transformer并行估计续看概率和条件奖励，在满足工业延迟约束的同时显著提升列表重排序效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统广泛采用生成-评估（G-E）框架进行重排序，但在顺序平台（如短视频）中用户是连续消费内容的，传统评估器通过聚合逐点值来打分列表，隐含假设曝光独立性，无法捕捉上下文依赖、用户连续性以及重复内容导致的边际效用递减等关键的会话级动态。

Method: 提出SWIM列表级评估器，将用户行为建模为有限时域前缀会话级生存过程。具体地，SWIM通过将当前列表对会话级目标的贡献分解为递归生存分布和到达位置条件奖励两部分来估计前缀条件贡献，并利用因果掩码Transformer并行估计续看概率和效用，以满足严格的工业延迟约束。

Result: 大量实验表明，SWIM在列表级重排序任务上显著优于基线方法，在整体推荐参与度上带来了实质性提升。

Conclusion: SWIM通过将会话级生存过程引入列表级评估，有效弥合了传统逐点估计与真实会话级用户行为之间的差距，在工业场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems have increasingly adopted the Generator-Evaluator (G-E) framework for the re-ranking stage. Within this paradigm, the generator produces candidate item lists from a pool filtered by upstream retrieval and ranking modules, while the evaluator scores these lists and selects the highest-scoring one for final exposure per request. However, on sequential platforms (e.g., short-video apps), users consume items continuously, ignoring artificial list boundaries. Conventional evaluators score lists by aggregating point-wise values, implicitly assuming exposure independence. This fails to capture critical session-level dynamics, such as contextual dependencies, user continuation, and diminishing marginal utility from repetitive content.
  To bridge this gap, we propose SWIM (Step-Wise Integrated Measure), a list-level evaluator that models user behaviors as a finite-horizon prefix session-level survival process. SWIM estimates the prefix-conditioned contribution of the current list to the session-level objective by factorizing it into a recursive survival distribution and reached-position conditional rewards. Leveraging a causally-masked Transformer, SWIM efficiently estimates continuation probabilities and utilities in parallel, satisfying strict industrial latency constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIM significantly outperforms baselines in listwise reranking tasks, yielding substantial improvements in overall recommendation engagement.

</details>


### [26] [D3ER: Supporting Multi-Modal Recommendation via Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25737)
*Bingnan Wang,Yi Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Jiangmeng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出D3ER方法，首次将梯度提升引入多模态推荐，通过交替学习模态同质判别信息和模态异质判别信息，并结合知识蒸馏与全局校正正则化，解决了现有方法中两类信息联合学习相互削弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态推荐方法虽然尝试提取各模态的判别性信息，但联合学习模态同质判别信息(HOI)与模态异质判别信息(HEI)会削弱它们各自的有效性，这是一个核心缺陷。

Method: 提出Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble (D3ER)方法：1) 首次将梯度提升引入多模态推荐，通过交替优化HOI和HEI的学习目标；2) 利用知识蒸馏降低梯度提升固有的高存储成本；3) 引入全局校正正则化避免局部最优问题。

Result: 在多个真实世界公开数据集上的实验验证了D3ER方法在多模态推荐任务中的优越性能。

Conclusion: D3ER通过梯度提升框架实现HOI与HEI的专业化分工学习，配合知识蒸馏和全局校正正则化，有效克服了传统联合学习的相互干扰问题，为多模态推荐提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Incorporating items' information shared among multiple modalities into a fused representation, multi-modal recommendation (MR) has demonstrated documented success than canonical unimodal recommendation. Although several attempts have been made to extract the discriminative information unique in each modality, existing methods suffer from a core limitation: the joint learning of modal-homogeneity discriminative information (HOI) and modal-heterogeneity discriminative information (HEI) tends to weaken their individual effectiveness. To remedy this deficiency, we propose a novel method, dubbed Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble for multi-modal Recommendation (D3ER). We introduce gradient boosting into MR for the first time to formalize the optimization objective for alternately learning HOI and HEI. This design enables models dedicated to each type of information to focus on their proficient samples, thereby promoting specialized optimization. Furthermore, to mitigate the inherent high storage cost and risk of local optima in gradient boosting, we enhance our framework with knowledge distillation and a global correction regularization. Experiments on prevalent real-world datasets confirm the superiority of our proposed method on MR.

</details>


### [27] [The "Curse of Knowledge" in LLM Query Simulation: Concept Provenance for Tracing Answer-Side Intrusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25245)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: LLM-generated search queries are widely used to augment IR evaluation, yet they may contain concepts that presuppose answer-side document knowledge, violating the information-access boundary of pre-search users. Existing validation metrics, including overlap, diversity, and effectiveness, cannot distinguish rare human-tail variation from candidate answer-side intrusion. We introduce concept provenance, a framework that assigns query concepts to backstory-supported, human-central, human-tail, and candidate answer-side zones, operationalizing a boundary that retrieval metrics alone cannot detect. Applying concept provenance to 77,004 queries across 100 UQV100 topics, 8 LLMs, and 5 prompt conditions with two extraction pipelines, we obtain a cross-pipeline token-HCIR Spearman rho of 1.0 over five condition means. Candidate answer-side concepts constitute 7.40 percent of non-generic concepts and appear in 97 of 100 topics, with topic explaining approximately 67 percent of variance. Human validation yields 68.2 percent relaxed precision, revealing two mechanisms: knowledge intrusion at 45.5 percent and deployment intrusion at 45.0 percent. Diagnostic probes show disproportionate localized retrieval effects, with deletion effect size d = -0.47 compared with d = -0.34 for random deletion, but these concepts explain less than 2 percent of aggregate evaluation variance. Concept provenance therefore serves as a boundary-compliance diagnostic rather than an evaluation-shift predictor. Under the tested conditions, no prompt condition eliminates intrusion; post-generation concept-provenance selection achieves 99 percent elimination.

</details>


### [28] [Rank-Deviation Quality: A Distance-Aware Metric for Multi-Answer Retrieval and Ranking Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25318)
*Xiaokun Zhou,Alessandro Moschitti,Danielle Class*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的检索与排序系统评估指标Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ)，能够适应查询中参考项数量不固定的场景（从单一正确答案到多个有效结果）。RDQ基于有序参考列表(ORL)对候选排序进行评分，通过输出位置权重和排序偏差惩罚来综合考虑"返回了哪些项"和"排序是否正确"两个维度，在5,000条查询的POI数据集上取得了最高的经验效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标存在不足：需要绝对相关性等级的指标标注成本高，而仅考虑排序相关性的指标（如Kendall's tau）忽略了"返回了哪些项目"这一维度，且难以适应不同查询具有不同数量参考项的场景。

Method: RDQ指标的核心设计：1) 使用有序参考列表(ORL)作为标注基础，支持成对或列表式判断；2) 每个检索到的参考项贡献"输出位置权重 × 排序偏差惩罚"；3) 不在ORL中的项目计零分；4) 应用程序特定参数控制对排序错乱的容忍度；5) 输出位置权重可反映界面可见性（如垂直列表或轮播图）。

Result: 在5,000条查询的POI数据集上，RDQ在13种评估指标配置中具有最高的中位数经验效能(power@100)。RDQ在200条查询时达到与自身全量查询排序tau≥0.8的一致性，而最强非RDQ配置RBP(0.9)需要250条查询。在TREC Deep Learning基准上，RDQ在n=25时与NDCG达到相当的中位效能，但NDCG在n=100时更高。

Conclusion: RDQ为检索和排序系统的评估提供了一种灵活且高效的替代方案，特别适用于参考项数量变化较大的场景。它既考虑了召回的项目集合，又考虑了排序质量，且在样本效率上优于现有指标。

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ), an evaluation metric for retrieval and ranking systems that adapts to queries with varying numbers of reference items, from a single correct answer to many valid results. RDQ scores a candidate ranking against an ordered reference list (ORL): each retrieved reference item contributes its output-position weight multiplied by a rank-deviation penalty, and items outside the ORL receive zero credit. Application-specific parameters control tolerance to misordering. Larger values emphasize retrieving valid reference items, whereas smaller values place more weight on matching their reference order. The output-position weights can reflect visibility in the application's interface, such as a vertical list or a carousel. Unlike metrics that require absolute relevance grades, RDQ operates on ordinal rankings, which annotators can produce through pairwise or listwise judgments. Unlike rank-correlation measures such as Kendall's tau, RDQ accounts for both which items are returned and how they are ordered. On a 5,000-query point-of-interest (POI) dataset with 12 systems, RDQ has the highest median empirical power@100 among the 13 evaluated metric configurations. It reaches mean tau >= 0.8 agreement with its own full-query ordering at 200 queries; RBP(0.9), the strongest tested non-RDQ configuration, reaches the same threshold at 250. On TREC Deep Learning benchmarks, where NDCG uses native graded labels and RDQ uses ordinal tiers derived from them, RDQ reaches comparable median power at n=25, while NDCG is higher at n=100.

</details>


### [29] [CRAMER: Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25370)
*Zhiyuan Julian Su,Naihe Feng,Zhen Luther Qin,Ga Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAMER框架，通过请求感知掩码（Request-Aware Masking）将用户自然语言请求作为控制信号，对冻结的骨干参数进行调制，实现对序列推荐模型的即时行为调整，避免了昂贵的重训练和大语言模型推理开销，在多个大规模基准数据集上优于四种最先进的请求感知基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐模型虽然强大，但在响应用户即时请求方面缺乏灵活性，难以适应用户的实时兴趣。现有方法要么需要重训练整个骨干网络，要么依赖大语言模型的提示工程推理能力，计算开销高昂，限制了其在大规模推荐服务中的应用。

Method: 受模型控制理论启发，CRAMER将用户自然语言请求视为控制信号，通过掩码机制调制冻结的骨干网络参数，实现多样请求的即时适应。该方法避免了昂贵的重训练，同时不需要调用大语言模型的推理能力。

Result: 在多个大规模基准数据集上的实验表明，CRAMER在多个推荐指标上优于四种最先进的请求感知基线方法，同时保持最小的计算开销。此外，该框架展现出增强的可控性和跨领域适应性。

Conclusion: CRAMER建立了请求感知序列推荐的新范式，通过掩码控制实现高效、即时、可跨领域适应的用户请求响应机制，在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models, while powerful, have limited flexibility in responding to immediate user requests, making it difficult to adapt their recommendations to the user's timely interests. Unfortunately, existing user request adaptation methods often incur high computational overhead due to either 1) retraining the entire backbone network or 2) leveraging the inference ability of large language models (a.k.a. prompt engineering), limiting their applicability in large-scale recommendation services. This paper presents Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders (CRAMER), a framework that takes users' natural-language requests to immediately change sequential recommendation models' behavior. Specifically, inspired by the model control theory, CRAMER treats user requests as control signals to modulate frozen backbone parameters through masking, achieving instant adaptation to diverse requests while avoiding costly retraining. Experiments on multiple large-scale benchmark datasets show that CRAMER outperforms four state-of-the-art request-aware baselines across multiple recommendation metrics while achieving minimal overhead. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits enhanced controllability and cross-domain adaptability, establishing a new paradigm for request-aware sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [30] [MOTIF: Motivation-guided Topology Inference for Cold-start Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25381)
*Yurui Shi,Yuchen Miao,Ximing Hu,Zijun Wang,Chang Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出MOTIF框架，通过动机语义推理、知识增强图重构、加权图对比学习和语义-结构对齐，解决冷启动多模态推荐中的交互稀疏、拓扑孤立和语义漂移三大耦合挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动多模态推荐面临三个相互耦合的挑战：(i)稀疏交互难以揭示用户意图，(ii)冷物品在拓扑上孤立，(iii)基于相似度的物品图可能导致语义漂移。现有方法难以同时应对这三个问题。

Method: MOTIF框架包含四个核心组件：语义动机推理（利用离线LLM推理推断动机语义）、知识增强图重构（重建可迁移的物品-物品拓扑）、加权图对比学习（学习鲁棒的图嵌入）、语义-结构对齐（将语义与结构信息对齐）。关键设计在于不将生成的文本注入预测过程，避免噪声传播。

Result: 在三个多模态基准数据集上的实验表明，MOTIF在基于图、多模态、冷启动和LLM增强的基线方法上均取得一致提升，相比最强近期基线最高有6.07%的相对改进。

Conclusion: MOTIF通过将LLM推理能力与图结构学习相结合，在不直接注入生成文本的情况下有效解决了冷启动多模态推荐的三大耦合挑战，验证了动机引导的拓扑推断框架的优越性。

Abstract: Cold-start multimodal recommendation faces three coupled challenges: (i) sparse interactions obscure user intent, (ii) cold items remain topologically isolated, and (iii) similarity-based item graphs may cause semantic drift. To address these issues, we propose MOTIF, a Motivation-guided Topology Inference framework for cold-start multimodal recommendation. MOTIF integrates Semantic Motivation Reasoning, Knowledge-enhanced Graph Reconstruction, Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning, and Semantic-Structural Alignment. It uses offline LLM reasoning to infer motivation semantics, reconstructs transferable item-item topology, and learns robust graph embeddings without injecting generated text into prediction. Experiments on three multimodal benchmarks show consistent gains over graph-based, multimodal, cold-start, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with up to 6.07% relative improvement over the strongest recent baseline.

</details>


### [31] [DocPC: Document-Level Visual Retrieval via Representative Page Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25434)
*Chengsong You,Junwei Zhou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DocPC，一种基于代表性页面合成的文档级视觉检索框架。它选取代表性页面并将其合成为单张网格图像进行文档级索引，显著减少索引图像、向量和存储（10.1倍）以及索引时间（约7.7倍）。同时引入多正样本对比学习与稀疏调度的列表优化，并发布带有多正相关标注的DocViRe基准。DocPC-ColQwen在DocViRe上NDCG@5达44.09，优于最强页面级基线38.91，存储减少10.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法以页面为中心，与需要完整文档检索的真实场景不匹配。天真的先页面后文档聚合策略存在线性索引成本高、跨页相关性检索质量下降的问题，亟需文档级视觉检索方案。

Method: 1）从文档中选取代表性页面并合成为单张网格图像，实现文档级索引；2）结合多正样本对比学习与稀疏调度的列表优化，处理文档级多正样本监督；3）构建带有多正相关标注的DocViRe基准。

Result: DocPC将索引图像、向量和存储减少10.1倍，端到端索引时间约缩短7.7倍；DocPC-ColQwen在DocViRe基准上NDCG@5达44.09，优于最强页面级基线38.91。

Conclusion: DocPC通过代表性页面合成实现了高效且高质量的文档级视觉检索，在显著降低存储和索引开销的同时提升了检索性能，验证了文档级范式相较于页面级范式的优势。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has advanced by encoding page screenshots with vision-language models, bypassing OCR pipelines. However, existing methods remain page-centric, misaligned with real-world scenarios requiring complete document retrieval. A naive page-then-document aggregation suffers from linear indexing cost and degraded retrieval when relevance spans multiple pages. To handle multi-positive supervision prevalent at the document level, we combine multi-positive contrastive learning with sparsely scheduled listwise optimization. We also introduce DocViRe, a benchmark with multi-positive relevance annotations. DocPC-ColQwen achieves NDCG@5 of 44.09 on DocViRe, outperforming the strongest page-level baseline at 38.91 while reducing storage by 10.1x. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DocPC-Document-Level-Visual-Retrieval-via-Representative-Page-Composition-1D52. Data is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/docpc.

TL;DR: 本文提出AnchorQE，一种无需训练的方法，通过分别编码原始查询和扩展查询再进行插值融合，并在无标签测试流上通过无监督在线策略估计插值权重，有效解决了LLM查询扩展在冻结稠密检索器上性能下降的问题。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在TREC-DL、LoTTE和BEIR数据集上，AnchorQE相比广泛使用的仅扩展或文本级拼接基线，检索效果最高提升12.89%。此外，在线估计插值权重的方法相比在开发集上调优的固定权重，最高提升3.81%。

Conclusion: 查询扩展的整合方式（而非仅扩展内容）是影响冻结稠密检索器性能的关键因素。AnchorQE通过分离编码与自适应插值的策略，提供了一种无需训练、无需人工标注的高效查询扩展整合方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate query expansions without task-specific training, yet the same expansions often make a frozen dense retriever worse. We identify an underexplored factor: prior work has often focused on what text is generated, while how generated text is incorporated into dense retrievers has received less systematic attention. By holding generated expansions fixed, we show that performance degradation can often be attributed to the integration method itself. We introduce AnchorQE, a training-free method that separately encodes the original query and its expansion before interpolating them. The interpolation factor is estimated using an unsupervised online strategy that operates over a small part of the unlabeled test stream. Intuitively, our strategy assigns high expansion trust only when expansions are both retrieval-strong and consistent with the original query's retrieved evidence. We show that AnchorQE improves retrieval effectiveness by up to 12.89% when compared to widely-used expansion-only or text-level concatenation baselines across TREC-DL, LoTTE, and BEIR. Further, we show that our online strategy to estimate the interpolation factor outperforms a fixed weight tuned on a development partition by up to 3.81%.

</details>

*Zhifei Zheng,Yunfei Liu,Bin Liu,Qiren Zhu,Hanbing Liu,Ziru Xu,Han Zhu,Jian Xu,Qi Qi,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TransRetrieval，一个可扩展的基于Transformer的推荐检索框架，通过加权平均聚合、目标token压缩和位置式域嵌入三大关键设计，解决了特征异构性导致的扩展瓶颈，在工业级数据集上实现计算规模与召回率的稳定对数线性扩展，并在线A/B测试中提升平台收入2.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐检索领域应用缩放定律面临特征异构性挑战：简单的堆叠Transformer层会导致收益递减，因为异构特征字段造成严重的token范数发散，阻碍了模型随计算预算和跨域数据扩展的能力。

Method: (1) 加权平均聚合：恢复Transformer所依赖的同质token假设；(2) 目标token压缩：将每个候选的FLOPs减少85%的同时保留交叉注意力表达能力；(3) 位置式域嵌入：以极低的额外成本统一多个域，将跨域数据转化为扩展资产。

Result: 在400亿交互的工业数据集和公开KuaiRand基准上，将每个目标的计算从0.1扩展到2 MFLOPs，Recall@2000提升+19.3/+22.2个百分点，验证了稳健的log-linear缩放规律；在线A/B测试中，在与生产基线相同的端到端延迟约束下，平台收入提升2.53%。

Conclusion: TransRetrieval通过解决特征异构性问题，成功将缩放定律应用于推荐检索领域，实现了计算预算和跨域数据的有效扩展，同时在实际生产环境中带来显著的收入增长，证明了该方法的实用价值与可扩展性。

Abstract: Applying scaling laws to recommendation retrieval is hindered by feature heterogeneity: naively stacking Transformer layers yields diminishing returns because heterogeneous fields produce severe token-norm divergence. We present TransRetrieval, a Transformer-based retrieval framework that scales with both computational budget and cross-domain data. The key enabler is (1) weighted average aggregation, which restores the homogeneous-token assumption Transformers rely on. Building on this, we introduce (2) target token compression that cuts per-candidate FLOPs by 85% while preserving cross-attention expressiveness, and (3) position-style domain embeddings that unify multiple domains at negligible additional cost, turning cross-domain data into a scaling asset. On a 40-billion-interaction industrial dataset and the public KuaiRand benchmark, scaling compute from 0.1 to 2 MFLOPs per target yields +19.3/+22.2 pt Recall@2000, confirming robust log-linear scaling. In online A/B tests, TransRetrieval lifts platform revenue by 2.53% under the same end-to-end latency constraint as the production baseline.

</details>
