<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM对文本文档的引用，将引用仅视为验证工具。但学术引用还承担信用和溯源两大功能，且同样适用于数据。作者认为数据引用于LLM是一个不同于文档级引用且更难解决的开放挑战。

Method: 通过概念分析论证数据引用对LLM的独特挑战，提出三个研究方向：（1）训练数据归因——将影响力估计转变为对参数化语料库的引用；（2）推理时数据引用——在正确的粒度和固定性级别识别数据集、子集和查询结果；（3）知识图谱事实引用——定义单个三元组引用的语义及信用沿溯源的传播机制。

Result: 本文为立场性论文，主要贡献在于系统性地框定了LLM数据引用问题，识别出三个关键研究方向，并呼吁跨社区合作。未报告具体的实验或实证结果。

Conclusion: LLM的数据引用需要同时满足可验证性、溯源可追踪性和信用到达数据创建者与管理者三个目标。三个方向的推进依赖于数据库、信息检索、知识表示和人工智能社区的联合研究。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly mediate access to information, and a growing body of work asks whether they cite the sources behind their outputs. That work treats citation as a verification device and applies it to textual documents. Scholarly citation serves two further functions, credit and provenance, and it applies to data as much as to text. This paper argues that data citation for large language models is an open challenge, distinct from document-level citation grounding and harder to solve. We ask how such models should cite data so that outputs stay verifiable, provenance stays traceable, and credit reaches data creators and curators. We set out three research directions. Training data attribution has to turn influence estimates into references for corpora absorbed into model parameters. Data citation at inference time has to identify datasets, subsets, and query results at the right granularity and fixity. Citing knowledge graph facts has to define what a reference to a single triple denotes and how credit propagates along provenance. Progress on all three depends on joint work across the database, information retrieval, knowledge representation, and artificial intelligence communities.

</details>


### [35] [An Event is Worth One Token: Event Tokenization for Industrial-scale LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25546)
*Fan Xia,Zhaoheng Zheng,Iman Setayesh,Ruogu Lin,Yiqin Pan,Samarth Mittal,Wentao Bao,Vinti Pandey,Sachin Patil,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao,Zhuang Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Sri Reddy,Minghai Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出事件中心（event-centric）的推荐范式，将每次交互表示为完整的时序快照，并引入新的扩展维度——快照分辨率（snapshot resolution），即每个事件编码的信息量。作者提出AMBER模型，通过瓶颈事件表示将时序快照压缩为紧凑的Event Token（一种新的LLM输入模态），在工业规模推荐任务上推进了计算-质量帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐系统虽然在模型容量和序列长度上不断扩展，但每个位置仅编码文本、语义ID或少量类别特征，忽略了每次事件中丰富的用户、物品、上下文和结果信号。在自回归建模下，这导致每个位置产生弱查询，且由于每个位置成为下一个位置的上下文，退化会沿着序列累积放大。

Method: AMBER的核心思路是：1）将每个交互表示为完整的时序快照（temporal snapshot）；2）通过瓶颈事件表示（Bottlenecked Event Representation）将快照压缩为紧凑的Event Token，作为新的LLM输入模态；3）表示学习端到端完成，但Event Token在服务时预计算并缓存，从而将快照分辨率与现实推理计算解耦。统一的tokenizer可跨不同实体类型使用。

Result: 在工业规模排序和检索基准上，AMBER相较于其他推荐范式推进了计算-质量帕累托前沿；在足够容量下，统一tokenizer优于专用per-entity tokenizer，显示跨异构实体类型的正向迁移；Event Token还可跨模型架构迁移——集成到非LLM排序器中作为服务时历史特征，带来统计显著的提升；进一步扩展Event Tokenizer容量可获得更多改进。

Conclusion: AMBER验证了事件编码信息量（快照分辨率）是推荐系统中有价值的新扩展维度，Event Token作为一种新的LLM输入模态，不仅能提升LLM推荐的性能，还能迁移到传统非LLM排序器中，展示了跨架构、跨实体类型的良好泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM-based recommendation has scaled along model capacity and sequence length, yet each position encodes only text, semantic IDs, or a few categorical features, discarding rich user, item, context, and outcome signals available at each event. Under autoregressive modeling, this yields weak queries at each position and, since each position becomes context for the next, the degradation compounds across the sequence. We propose an event-centric paradigm that represents each interaction by its full temporal snapshot, and identify a new scaling dimension we term snapshot resolution: the amount of information encoded per event. To efficiently scale snapshot resolution, we introduce AMBER (Autoregressive Modeling via Bottlenecked Event Representation), which compresses each temporal snapshot into a compact Event Token, a new LLM input modality. The representation is learned end-to-end, while Event Tokens are pre-computed and cached for serving, decoupling snapshot resolution from real-time serving compute. On industrial-scale ranking and retrieval benchmarks, AMBER advances the compute-quality Pareto frontier relative to alternative recommendation paradigms. At sufficient capacity, a single unified tokenizer even outperforms dedicated per-entity tokenizers, demonstrating positive transfer across structurally different entity types. AMBER's Event Tokens also transfer across model architectures: when integrated into a heavily optimized non-LLM ranker as serving-time historical features, they yield statistically significant improvements. Further scaling Event Tokenizer capacity provides additional improvements.

</details>


### [36] [When Stale Constraints Go Unchecked: Budgeted Verification Failures in Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25553)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体继承"已合并记忆"时可能因记录被更新而持有过时约束的问题。在固定验证预算（两条记录）下，研究者将记忆形式、世界状态（当前记录或已取代记录）及验证策略进行显式建模，对比原生分配与将一条验证槽位分配给关键溯源路径的效果。结果显示：约束被取代时，原生分配在三种场景下产生74.7%~77.3%的"陈旧一致"决策；而将一条槽位重新分配给关键路径后，决策正确性提升60~74个百分点，且在六个模型中均为正向效果。研究还报告了因时间不一致而修正后的稳健性复现。结论是记忆系统可能需要区分"新鲜度"与"相关性"的独立信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当一个智能体继承了某个已合并的记忆，该记忆中所隐含的约束可能在写入时成立，但之后被更新的权威记录取代。在稀缺的验证预算下，智能体能否发现这一取代？如果不能，是否可以在不增加预算的情况下避免该错误？这是本文要回答的核心问题。

Method: 对"取代"进行显式建模：历史溯源不可变，变化的只是哪条记录是当前有效的。通过设计赋予记忆形式、世界状态（源为当前或已取代）及固定预算两条记录的验证策略。对比三种方案：智能体自身分配、将一条槽位分配给关键溯源路径、或分配给随机记录。

Result: 约束已声明时，智能体仅在大约1/5的episode中检查其溯源路径；当约束被取代时，原生分配在初次运行、新措辞复现及保留域中分别产生77.3%、74.7%、74.7%的陈旧一致决策。将一条验证槽位重新分配给关键路径后，正确决策率分别提升+74.0、+72.7和+61.3个百分点，在三个运行中六个模型全部正向。当记录与记忆一致时，该干预无任何影响。稳健性复现（修正一处时间不一致后）得到+73.3个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 该干预利用了关键路径的知识，并非调度器。它表明陈旧记忆错误中可归因于验证分配的部分已接近其结构性上限。记忆系统可能需要独立于"相关性"的"新鲜度"或"取代"信号。

Abstract: An agent that inherits a consolidated memory may inherit a constraint that was true when written and has since been withdrawn by a newer authoritative record. Under a scarce verification budget, does the agent recover the withdrawal, and if not, is the error avoidable without spending more? We model supersession explicitly -- historical provenance is immutable; what changes is which record is current -- and assign by design the memory's form, the world's state (source current or superseded), and the verification policy at a fixed budget of two records: the agent's own allocation, or the same budget with one slot re-assigned to the critical provenance path or to a random record. With a constraint stated, agents inspected its provenance path in about one episode in five; when that constraint had been superseded, native allocation produced stale-consistent decisions in 77.3%, 74.7% and 74.7% of episodes across a primary run, a fresh-wording replication and a held-out domain. Re-assigning one slot to the critical path raised current-record-consistent decisions by +74.0, +72.7 and +61.3 points, positive in six of six models in each of those runs, and changed nothing when the record agreed with the memory. The held-out scenario was later found to contain a temporal inconsistency; a robustness replication with one sentence corrected, deposited externally before execution, gave +73.3 points and is reported alongside the original. The intervention uses knowledge of the critical path and is not a scheduler; it identifies that the share of stale-memory error attributable to verification allocation is close to its structural ceiling. Memory systems may need freshness or supersession signals separate from relevance.

</details>


### [37] [RetrievalRouter: Joint Modality and Architecture Selection for Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25625)
*Emre Kuru,Mehmet Onur Keskin,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出RetrievalRouter，一种轻量级的查询感知路由器，能够根据查询文本自动选择最适合的检索管道（dense或late-interaction），通过单一可调参数暴露完整的准确率-延迟权衡前沿。实验表明，相比最佳静态基线，RetrievalRouter在准确率上提升2.5%，速度提升12.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索管道在模态（文本或多模态）和架构（dense或late-interaction）上的选择存在硬性权衡：最高效的管道速度慢且成本高，最快的管道无法从复杂文档中检索证据。实践者被迫在遗漏证据和不可用的延迟之间做选择，且缺乏基于查询级别的原则性决策依据。

Method: RetrievalRouter是一个轻量级的查询感知路由器，仅从查询文本中学习哪个检索管道最适合每个查询。系统通过单一可调参数暴露完整的准确率-延迟前沿，允许用户在两者之间灵活权衡。

Result: 在金融和科学语料库的基准测试中，RetrievalRouter相比最佳静态基线，准确率提升2.5%，速度快12.4倍。相比先前自适应策略选择方法，在面向准确率的设置中nDCG@5显著更高，在面向延迟的设置中nDCG@5和延迟均匹配或数值上更优。

Conclusion: 静态管道并非在所有查询上都是最优的，查询级自适应路由是一种更优的检索策略。RetrievalRouter证明了这一妥协是不必要的——通过查询感知的路由，可以在不牺牲准确率的情况下获得更快的检索速度。

Abstract: Document retrieval increasingly supports high-stakes information access in finance, healthcare, and law. Modern retrieval pipelines vary both in modality (text or multimodal) and in retrieval architecture (dense or late-interaction). These choices impose a hard compromise: the most effective pipelines are too slow and expensive to run at scale, while the fastest fail to retrieve evidence from complex documents. Practitioners must therefore choose between missed evidence and unusable latency, with no principled basis for adapting that choice at the query level. We show that this compromise is unnecessary. Not every query requires the same pipeline. Across benchmarks spanning financial and scientific corpora, no static pipeline dominates. We introduce RetrievalRouter, a lightweight query-aware router that learns, from the query text alone, which retrieval pipeline best fits each query. A single tunable parameter exposes the full accuracy-latency frontier, and for every static baseline, RetrievalRouter offers an operating point that is simultaneously more accurate and faster. Against the best static baseline, RetrievalRouter is 2.5% more accurate and 12.4 times faster. Furthermore, compared with prior adaptive strategy selection methods, RetrievalRouter achieves significantly higher nDCG@5 across accuracy-oriented settings, while matching or numerically outperforming them on both nDCG@5 and latency in latency-oriented settings. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/emrekuruu/retrieval-router.

</details>


### [38] [Hamiltonian Spectral-Temporal Dissipative Dynamics for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25755)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR)，将序列推荐建模为耗散哈密顿系统（二阶动力学），在频域中求解闭式解，并通过可学习的耗散机制和局部脉冲精炼模块分别捕捉兴趣衰减和突然行为波动，在三个基准数据集上超越现有Transformer和状态空间模型类推荐器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型大多将用户偏好演化视为一阶过程（下一状态仅依赖当前潜在表示），但真实用户行为包含惯性、周期性和突变等更丰富的动力学特征，一阶假设无法完整捕捉这些现象。

Method: 将偏好演化重构为耗散哈密顿系统，在潜在相空间中引入位置（稳定偏好）和动量（短期倾向）两个变量；利用线性时不变结构的频域闭式解进行建模；设计可学习耗散机制捕获兴趣自然衰减，并通过局部脉冲精炼模块处理稀疏交互日志中的突然行为波动。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HSR在性能上持续优于最先进的基于Transformer和基于状态空间模型（SSM）的推荐系统。

Conclusion: 通过二阶动力学系统视角联合建模全局周期模式、惯性演化和局部冲击三类现象，HSR为序列推荐提供了超越一阶假设的新范式。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation requires understanding how user preferences evolve over time, yet most existing models treat such evolution as a first order process where the next state depends solely on the current latent representation. Nevertheless, real user behavior often exhibits richer dynamics, including inertia, periodicity, and sudden shifts that cannot be fully captured by these first order assumptions. Motivated by these behavioral characteristics, we reconceptualize sequential recommendation through the lens of second order dynamical systems and introduce the Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR), which recasts preference evolution as a dissipative Hamiltonian system in a latent phase space of position (stable preference) and momentum (short-term tendency). The linear time-invariant structure of the governing equation admits a closed-form solution in the frequency domain. A learnable dissipation mechanism further captures natural interest decay, while a short local impulse refinement module models abrupt behavioral fluctuations commonly observed in sparse interaction logs. This design jointly accounts for global periodic patterns, inertial evolution, and localized shocks, where three phenomena that are underrepresented in existing sequential models. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Transformer-based and state space model (SSM)-based recommenders.

</details>


### [39] [PUMA: Post-Hoc Sparsification of Universal Multimodal Embeddings for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25780)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Franco Maria Nardini,Claudio Pomo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PUMA是一种稀疏自编码器方案，可将通用多模态嵌入转换为紧凑稀疏编码，无需重新训练骨干网络，在保持或提升检索性能的同时将向量存储减少8-16倍，推理速度最高提升25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入能实现跨文本、图像及组合查询的检索，但其稠密表示带来高昂的内存和推理成本。事后稀疏化可降低这些成本，但在多模态检索领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: PUMA采用两阶段稀疏自编码器方案：预训练阶段保持稠密点积几何结构，随后对稀疏编码器进行检索任务的微调，从而在不重新训练骨干网络的前提下将多模态嵌入映射为紧凑稀疏编码。

Result: 在5个基准（涵盖文生图检索和组合图像检索）上评估，基于Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B，PUMA在4/5数据集上与稠密检索统计上无差异或更优。向量存储减少8-16倍（FP32），在更大候选池上比精确稠密评分快最多25倍。

Conclusion: PUMA实现了高效的多模态检索，并识别出事后稀疏化的两种失败模式：pre-TopK支持不足和检索对齐的活动支持不足，为后续改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedders enable retrieval across text, image, and combined queries, but their dense representations incur high memory and inference costs. Post-hoc sparsification could reduce these costs but remains underexplored for multimodal retrieval. We introduce PUMA, a sparse autoencoder recipe that maps universal multimodal embeddings to compact sparse codes without retraining the backbone: a pretraining stage preserves dense dot-product geometry, after which the sparse encoder is fine-tuned for retrieval. We evaluate on five benchmarks covering text-to-image and composed image retrieval. On Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B, PUMA is statistically indistinguishable from or improves over dense retrieval on four of five datasets. We further identify two failure modes of post-hoc sparsification: insufficient pre-TopK support and retrieval-misaligned active support. PUMA reduces vector storage by 8-16x (FP32) and is up to 25x faster than exact dense scoring on larger candidate pools, enabling efficient multimodal retrieval.

</details>

*Nabaraj Subedi,Shuvo Dip Datta,Ahmed Abdelaty,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.IR

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统土木基础设施合规检查依赖工程师人工阅读二维图纸，而基于OCR的自动化方案会丢失图纸中关键的几何信息和布局信息，无法有效解释平面图的语义内容，迫切需要一种能够直接处理视觉信息的自动化合规检查方法。

Method: 提出视觉优先的多模态RAG框架PlanSightRAG，包含三个核心组件：(1) ColNomic-3B多向量检索模块，直接对平面图图像进行索引；(2) 智能体化的Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer四阶段流水线；(3) MaxSim热力图作为证据轨迹。同时构建了5个州交通部标准的4,056对基准数据集。

Result: PlanSightRAG在零样本检索上达到91.47%的Recall@5，在密歇根州交通部held-out语料上达到91.40%。在合成参数化合规图纸上，Qwen2.5-VL-72B流水线仅在提供预解析规则阈值时达到100%判定准确率（对照非VLM OCR基线为76.4%）。此外成功实现了无需人工规则的自主视觉规则锚定。

Conclusion: PlanSightRAG验证了视觉优先的多模态RAG方法在工程图纸合规检查中的有效性，相比传统OCR方案有显著优势，但规则预解析仍是当前VLM管线达到完全准确率的关键前提，未来需进一步探索从规范语料中完全自动化的规则提取与锚定。

Abstract: Civil infrastructure compliance checking has long relied on engineers manually reading legacy 2D plans; however, OCR-based automation strips away the geometry and layout essential for interpreting these plans. We present a Visual-First Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) framework called PlanSightRAG. It indexes and reasons directly over plan imagery, integrates a ColNomic-3B multi-vector retrieval, an agentic Planner-Retriever-Auditor-Synthesizer, and MaxSim heatmaps as an evidence trail. We introduce a 4,056-pair benchmark from five state Departments of Transportation (DOT) standard plans (1,898 pages). PlanSightRAG achieves 91.47% Recall@5 on zero-shot retrieval, while on a held-out Michigan DOT corpus, it achieves 91.40%. On synthetic, parametrically-generated compliance drawings, our Qwen2.5-VL-72B pipeline reaches 100% verdict accuracy only when supplied a pre-resolved rule threshold, a controlled ceiling that a non-VLM OCR baseline already reaches at 76.4%. Finally, we demonstrate autonomous visual rule-grounding by extracting numeric limits directly from a specification corpus without any human-supplied rules.

</details>

### [41] [Probabilistic Performance Analysis of Parallel Signature Search Strategies in Multi-Level Tree Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25087)
*Jingwei Li,Thomas G. Robertazzi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个概率框架，用于在读取任何文件之前预测五种搜索策略（从顺序扫描到全树并行）在树形结构文件集合中完成分布式搜索的完成时间，模型考虑节点扫描时间混合分布、层时间顺序统计量、并行子树极值论证以及容量约束下的生成函数，并在毫秒级计算成本内给出带精度标签的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树形分布式搜索（如分布式索引遍历、深度包检测、序列比对）中，实践者需要在不同并行度策略间做选择，但现有分析仅部分回答该问题：要么只通过签名持有文件的统计特征刻画每个节点，要么对多签名文件需要运行时才能获得的信息，缺乏在运行前就能预测完成时间的通用框架。

Method: 建立概率框架预测五种搜索策略的完成时间。将节点扫描时间建模为签名存在性的混合分布，层时间用顺序统计量刻画，并行子树扫描用极值论证处理；当签名数量已知时，通过生成函数处理容量约束下的占位问题。每个性能公式携带精确性标签（精确、拟合、渐近或界限），并通过蒙特卡洛模拟量化近似误差并识别适用区间。

Result: 多核原型验证了全树、层和子树级并行之间的粗略区分，但发现同步开销可能消除相近策略之间的预测差异。框架在不到一毫秒的时间内完成了设计实例评估，提供了具有明确精度区间和可忽略计算成本的事前完成时间预测。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在文件读取前以极低计算成本给出带显式精度区间的完成时间预测，可支撑后续的资源成本优化决策。

Abstract: Hierarchical distributed search, locating a data pattern, or signature, across a tree-structured collection of files, underlies distributed index traversal, deep packet inspection and sequence alignment. A practitioner must decide how much parallelism to employ: scan each layer sequentially, fan out within subtrees, or launch the whole tree at once. Existing analyses answer this only partially: they characterize every node by the statistics of a signature-holding file and, for multi-signature files, need quantities revealed only at run time. We develop a probabilistic framework predicting the completion time of five search strategies, spanning sequential to full-tree parallelism, before any file is read. Node scan times are modeled as a mixture over signature presence, layer times as order statistics, and parallel subtree scans by extreme-value arguments; when signature counts are known, occupancy under capacity constraints is treated by generating functions. Each performance formula carries an exactness label: exact (or exact-in-regime), plug-in, asymptotic or bound, with each approximation quantified against Monte Carlo simulation and its regime identified. A multicore prototype reproduces the coarse separation between full-tree, layer- and subtree-level parallelism, but shows that synchronization overhead can erase the predicted separation between close strategies. The framework delivers a priori completion-time predictions with explicit accuracy regimes and negligible computational cost, the design example evaluated in under a millisecond; these timing models can support subsequent resource-cost optimization.

</details>


### [42] [Hierarchical Shared Memory-Aware Optimization for TRSM on GPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25469)
*Xinzhe Chen,Haowei Li,Lijuan Hu,Wenjing Ma,Fangfang Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HSMA-TRSM，一个面向NVIDIA A100、H800和Hygon DCU Z100加速器的分层共享内存感知TRSM优化框架。针对小规模问题设计流水线计算-内存重叠机制和双线程组七级流水线策略，针对大规模问题提出对角块解耦优化以实现O(IB)共享内存占用。相比cuBLAS和rocBLAS分别取得最高2.05倍和2.06倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRSM是基础BLAS Level-3操作，但GPU上左三角下三角情形因前向替换的严格行依赖和共享内存不足（尤其双复数宽数据类型）而难以高效实现，现有成熟厂商内核在部分场景优化空间有限。

Method: 提出分层共享内存感知优化框架：(1)小规模(m,n<=64)采用循环展开与指令重排实现流水线计算-内存重叠，并设计双线程组七级流水线策略解决双复数类型共享内存限制；(2)大规模问题采用对角块解耦优化，共享内存占用O(IB)，根据矩阵规模和硬件特性自适应选择块大小；(3)基于离线分析和在线查找的编译期配置选择框架，零运行时开销地选取最优块大小。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100、H800和Hygon DCU Z100上，HSMA-TRSM相比cuBLAS最高加速2.05倍，相比rocBLAS最高加速2.06倍。共享内存受限的双复数小规模场景和自适应分块的大规模实类型场景加速效果最强。

Conclusion: HSMA-TRSM通过分层感知共享内存约束的优化策略，在多种GPU架构上显著提升左三角TRSM性能，验证了针对不同规模问题定制优化方案的有效性，但成熟厂商内核在部分场景已接近最优，留给自定义优化的空间有限。

Abstract: Triangular Solve with Multiple Right-hand Sides (TRSM) is a fundamental BLAS Level-3 operation that underpins LU/Cholesky decomposition, sparse direct solvers, and matrix inversion. In the left-side lower-triangular case studied in this paper, efficient GPU implementation remains challenging because forward substitution introduces strict row-wise dependencies, and shared memory is too scarce to hold both operand matrices for wide data types such as double complex. This paper presents HSMA-TRSM, a hierarchical shared memory-aware optimization framework for left-side lower-triangular TRSM on NVIDIA A100, NVIDIA H800, and Hygon DCU Z100 accelerators. For the small-scale regime (m,n<=64), we design a pipelined compute-memory overlap mechanism through loop unrolling and instruction reordering, and propose a dual thread-group seven-stage pipeline strategy to address shared memory constraints for double complex types. For large-scale problems, we introduce a diagonal block decoupling optimization with an O(IB)shared-memory footprint for diagonal block inversion, enabling adaptive block size selection based on matrix scale and hardware characteristics. A compile-time configuration selection framework based on offline profiling and online lookup selects the optimal block size per platform with zero runtime overhead. Evaluated on NVIDIA A100, H800, and Hygon DCU Z100, HSMA-TRSM achieves peak speedups of 2.05xover cuBLAS and 2.06xover rocBLAS. The gains are strongest in shared-memory-constrained double-complex small cases and in large real-type cases where adaptive blocking improves GEMM-dominated updates, while mature vendor kernels leave less optimization headroom in some regimes.

</details>


### [43] [psRL: Efficient Training for Agentic AI via Training-Time Prefix Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25683)
*Mianjie Yu,Zizhao Mo,Huanyu Qu,Zhirong Qian,Huanle Xu,Cen Li,Zifeng Zhao,Zhi Zhou,Jinhua Zhou,Jun Xie,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了psRL，一个面向智能体AI训练的新型训练系统，利用更新阶段的数据不可变性和全局可见性，通过前缀共享机制和KV缓存管理，实现高效的工作负载调度和内存管理，在吞吐量上较现有系统最高提升5.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体AI训练中，系统瓶颈已从采样（rollout）转向更新（update）阶段。新兴采样策略（如树结构、逐步RL）大幅增加训练样本量但边际采样成本较低，导致更新阶段主导端到端执行时间。同时，生产环境迹线显示训练样本间存在显著的前缀冗余，这为优化提供了新的机会。

Method: psRL利用更新阶段固有的全局可见性和数据不可变性，提出两种新颖的前缀共享机制，实现GPU工作节点间灵活细粒度的工作负载分配，同时优化前缀复用和负载均衡。此外，实现了一个新的底层KV缓存管理器，支持可适应的块大小分配和动态KV缓存，最大化内存利用率并保持高前缀命中率。

Result: 使用生产迹线进行评估，psRL在吞吐量上较现有系统提升最高达5.2倍。

Conclusion: psRL通过前缀共享有效解决了智能体AI训练中更新阶段成为瓶颈的问题，同时兼顾负载均衡和内存效率，展示了利用更新阶段数据特性进行系统优化的巨大潜力。

Abstract: In modern agentic AI training, the system bottleneck is shifting from rollout to update. Emerging sampling strategies such as tree-structured and step-wise RL greatly increase training sample volume while incurring relatively low marginal rollout cost, causing the update phase to dominate the end-to-end execution time. Crucially, this shift exposes a new optimization opportunity, as production traces reveal substantial prefix redundancy across training samples. In this paper, we propose psRL (prefix sharing for RL), a new training system for agentic AI designed to exploit prefix redundancy among training samples. Leveraging the global visibility and data immutability inherent to the update phase, psRL achieves efficient workload scheduling and memory management for distributed training. Specifically, psRL introduces two novel prefix-sharing mechanisms that enable flexible, fine-grained workload distribution across GPU workers, simultaneously optimizing prefix reuse and achieving load balancing. Moreover, psRL implements a new underlying KV cache manager that facilitates adaptable block-size allocation and dynamic KV caching, maximizing memory utilization while maintaining a high prefix hit rate. Evaluations using production traces demonstrate that psRL outperforms existing systems by up to 5.2x in throughput. The source code will be publicly available soon.

</details>

*Deepika Saxena,Ashutosh Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向行业云提供商的云资源管理框架，通过资源超订和异构服务定价模型来最大化盈利和运营效率，利用自适应集成机器学习预测VM资源使用率，并结合Fuzzy C均值聚类和两种定价模型（延迟敏感模型和尽力而为模型）优化资源分配，显著降低电费、功耗和活跃服务器数量，同时提升资源利用率和利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行业云提供商面临资源浪费和运营成本高企的问题，现有方法未能充分结合资源超订、预测性资源管理和差异化定价策略来同时实现盈利最大化和运营效率提升。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于自适应集成机器学习的VM资源利用率预测模型，用于主动估算用户VM的资源使用；(2) Fuzzy C均值聚类将预测资源使用相似的VM分组，以确定部署所需VM数量和配置；(3) 两种定价模型（延迟敏感模型和尽力而为模型）对用户请求进行分类和VM选择，以最大化收入和降低电费。

Result: 使用两个基准VM轨迹数据集进行仿真实验，与最先进方法对比，电费降低55.56%，功耗降低高达60.7%，活跃服务器减少51%，资源利用率提升60%，利润提升51.18%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了所提出的行业云资源管理框架在降本增效方面的显著优势，证明了自适应机器学习预测、Fuzzy C均值聚类与异构定价模型相结合的策略能够有效提升行业云提供商的盈利能力和运营效率。

Abstract: This article proposed a novel industry cloud resource management framework that exploits resource oversubscription and heterogeneous service pricing models to maximize profitability and operational efficiency for industry cloud providers. The framework proposes an adaptive ensemble machine learning driven prediction model for proactive estimation of resource utilization of Virtual Machines (VM)s based on previous resource utilization of respective users' VMs to minimize resource wastage due to oversubscription by them. Accordingly, the VMs having similar predicted resource usage are grouped using Fuzzy C means clustering. This helps to determine the required number of VMs with specific configuration to be deployed before executing user requests. Concurrently, the framework incorporates two distinct categories of cloud service pricing models, namely the Delay Sensitive Model and the Best-Effort Model. Accordingly, the user requests are classified and executed by selecting the most suitable VMs, with the goal of maximizing revenue and reducing electricity costs in cloud data centers (CDCs). Experimental simulation and comparison against state-of-the-art methods, using two benchmark VM traces, validates the performance of proposed framework. It significantly reduces electricity bills by 55.56 percentage, power consumption and active servers by up to 60.7 percentage and 51 percentage, respectively, while improving resource utilization and profits by up to 60 percentage and 51.18 percentage, respectively

</details>

*Philipp Ungrund,Kurt Rothermel,Sukanya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MQTT5协议的扩展方案，在不违反标准且无需修改现有客户端的情况下，为发布-订阅模型增加空间过滤和路由能力，实现面向CPS/IoT的感知上下文的高效消息传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间和邻近感知是CPS和IoT中高效通信的关键使能因素，但缺乏适当的中间件支持和标准化的空间感知机制，严重限制了位置相关应用的普及。

Method: 扩展基于主题的发布-订阅模型，集成空间过滤和路由功能；采用灵活的几何方法描述空间过滤器，实现表达力强且高效的空间订阅；结合几何世界模型与增强的主题代理（broker），实现空间约束的消息传播；利用MQTT5协议实现，不违反标准且不需要修改现有客户端。

Result: 基于合成数据和真实数据的实验结果表明，该系统具有较高的效率，代理端计算开销极小，并且在实际IoT设备上部署具有可行性和实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持MQTT5标准兼容性的前提下，成功为现有IoT系统注入了空间感知能力，为位置相关应用的广泛应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Spatial and proximity awareness are critical enablers for efficient communication in Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) and the Internet of Things (IoT). However, the lack of suitable middleware support and standardized mechanisms for spatial awareness significantly limits the pervasiveness of location-dependent applications. In this paper, we present a novel approach that extends topic-based publish-subscribe, a dominant messaging model in this domain, with integrated spatial filtering and routing capabilities. Our approach leverages a highly flexible geometric method for describing spatial filters, enabling expressive and efficient spatial subscriptions. A geometric world model operates together with an enhanced topic-based broker to realize spatially constrained message dissemination. Importantly, this functionality is achieved using the MQTT5 protocol without violating the standard or requiring any modifications to existing client implementations. Experimental results based on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate strong system efficiency, minimal computational overhead at the broker, and the practical feasibility of deploying the approach on existing IoT devices.

</details>


### [46] [REE-TM: Reliable and Energy-Efficient Traffic Management Model for Diverse Cloud Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25747)
*Ashutosh Kumar Singh,Deepika Saxena,Volker Lindenstruth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出REE-TM框架，通过异构任务分类、TG-QNN工作量预测和QBHO权重优化，实现云环境中可靠且节能的流量管理，相比无REE-TM方案可靠性提升30.25%，能效提升23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有云资源管理研究对来自广泛互联网用户的异构作业请求特征考虑不足或忽视，导致资源分配和管理的效率受限。

Method: REE-TM框架包含三方面：(1) 对异构作业请求进行分类，并选择最合适的虚拟节点和物理节点执行；(2) 提出基于Toffoli门量子神经网络（TG-QNN）的工作量估计器，利用量子黑洞优化（QBHO）算法优化学习权重，用于预测工作负载并检测资源拥塞；(3) 通过信息熵计算和流量状态分析来识别潜在的资源拥塞风险。

Result: 在基准数据集上通过仿真评估，与无REE-TM版本相比，可靠性提升最高达30.25%，能效提升最高达23%。

Conclusion: REE-TM通过利用互联网流量的多样性特征和量子计算技术，有效提升了云环境下资源管理的可靠性和能源效率，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Diversity of workload demands lays a critical impact on efficient resource allocation and management of cloud services. The existing literature has either weakly considered or overlooked the heterogeneous feature of job requests received from wide range of internet services users. To address this context, the proposed approach named Reliable and Energy Efficient Traffic Management (REE-TM) has exploited the diversity of internet traffic in terms of variation in resource demands and expected complexity. Specifically, REE-TM incorporates categorization of heterogeneous job requests and executes them by selecting the most admissible virtual node (a software-defined instance such as a virtual machine or container) and physical node (an actual hardware server or compute host) within the cloud infrastructure. To deal with resource-contention-based resource failures and performance degradation, a novel workload estimator 'Toffoli Gate-based Quantum Neural Network' (TG-QNN) is proposed, wherein learning process or interconnection weights optimization is achieved using Quantum version of BlackHole (QBHO) algorithm. The proactively estimated workload is used to compute entropy of the upcoming internet traffic with various traffic states analysis for detection of probable resource-congestion. REE-TM is extensively evaluated through simulations using a benchmark dataset and compared with optimal and without REE-TM versions. The performance evaluation and comparison of REE-TM with measured significant metrics reveal its effectiveness in assuring higher reliability by up to 30.25% and energy-efficiency by up to 23% as compared without REE-TM.

</details>

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合量子力学与神经网络的Hadamard神经网络（QB-HNN），用于工业云工作负载预测与资源预留。通过量子黑洞双相优化算法（QB-BiO）训练权重，在六种基准数据集上相比LSTM和EQNN分别降低预测误差36.36%和22.83%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型处理多样化、高维且需求突变的云工作负载时学习能力有限，预测不准确导致功耗过高和资源管理问题，亟需更高效的预测模型。

Method: 提出Hadamard神经网络（QB-HNN），将工作负载信息转换为量子比特，通过Hadamard门激活函数的深层量子比特神经元网络传播，实现叠加态模式学习；同时引入量子黑洞双相优化算法（QB-BiO）训练和优化量子神经权重。

Result: 在六种异构云工作负载基准数据集上与五种先进方法对比，预测精度表现优异，相比LSTM和EQNN方法分别将预测误差降低36.36%和22.83%。

Conclusion: 所提出的QB-HNN模型能够有效应对云工作负载的多样性和突发性变化，显著提升预测精度，为工业云的资源预留和节能管理提供了有力的技术支持。

Abstract: Accurate workload prediction and proactive resource reservation are crucial for industry clouds. However, the conventional machine learning (CML) models with limited learning capabilities often fail to predict diverse, high-dimensional workloads with sudden changes in resource demand, leading to excessive power consumption and resource management issues. In this context, this article proposes a novel Hadamard neural network with quantum blackhole (QB-HNN) optimization. This model combines the computational efficiency of quantum mechanics with the persuasive learning capability of neural networks (NNs). The workload information is transformed into qubits and propagated via a deep network of qubit neurons comprising a Hadamard-gated activation function to fetch superposition within the QB-HNN model for intuitive pattern learning. Furthermore, a novel quantum blackhole biphase optimization (QB-BiO) algorithm is introduced to train and optimize qubit neural weights. The performance of the proposed model is comprehensively evaluated and compared with five state-of-the-art approaches using six benchmark datasets of three heterogeneous varieties of cloud workloads. The prediction accuracy achieved for an extensive range of workloads confirms its influential performance by minimizing the prediction error up to 36.36% and 22.83% over existing LSTM- and EQNN-based prediction approaches, respectively.

</details>

*Matvey Moisseyev,Huijing Du,Dandan Zheng,Chi Zhang,Hongfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于多GPU的可扩展3D多细胞生长模拟框架，结合GPU加速、空间分箱、域分解和负载感知分区，并引入基于RNN的负载均衡控制器来应对细胞运动、生长和分裂导致的动态工作负载分布变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于亚细胞元素模型（SEMs）的多细胞生长模拟能捕捉复杂的组织发育过程，但元素级交互带来巨大的计算开销。且细胞运动、生长和分裂持续改变空间工作负载分布，导致初始平衡的分区随时间变得低效。

Method: 提出可扩展的多GPU框架，综合运用GPU加速、空间分箱、域分解和工作负载感知分区。核心创新是RNN负载均衡控制器，它观察每秩执行时间和分区状态，学习对反应式边界调整规则的残差校正。控制器在可微的负载均衡循环代理中使用随机化工作负载动态进行离线训练，无需实测执行轨迹。

Result: GPU加速结合空间分箱使交互计算比串行CPU基线快约三个数量级。RNN引导的负载均衡将平均全局不平衡度从静态分区的11.3%降至3.5%，端到端运行时间较静态分区降低9.0%，切片迁移量较反应式基线减少7.7倍。

Conclusion: 历史感知的负载均衡控制能够在改善工作负载平衡的同时避免不必要的重分区，为大规模多细胞生长模拟提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Detailed multicellular growth simulations based on subcellular element models (SEMs) can capture complex tissue development, but their element-level interactions impose substantial computational cost. This work presents a scalable multi-GPU framework for 3D multicellular growth simulation that combines GPU acceleration, spatial binning, domain decomposition, and workload-aware partitioning. Cell movement, growth, and division continuously reshape the spatial workload distribution, causing initially balanced partitions to become inefficient over time. To address this, we introduce an RNN-based load-balancing controller that observes recent per-rank execution times and partition states and learns residual corrections to a reactive boundary-adjustment rule. The controller is trained offline in a differentiable surrogate of the load-balancing loop with randomized workload dynamics, requiring no measured execution traces for training. We evaluate the framework in terms of single-GPU acceleration, multi-GPU computation scaling, controller-level load-balancing behavior, and end-to-end simulation performance, with comparisons against static partitioning, reactive load balancing, and conventional time-series prediction baselines. A representative embryonic epidermal development use case further demonstrates the type of spatially and temporally evolving workload targeted by the framework. In our evaluation, GPU acceleration with spatial binning accelerates the interaction computation by roughly three orders of magnitude over a serial CPU baseline. RNN-guided load balancing reduces the mean global imbalance from 11.3% under static partitioning to 3.5%, lowers end-to-end runtime by 9.0% relative to static partitioning, and reduces slice migration by 7.7x compared with the reactive baseline, showing that history-aware control can improve workload balance while avoiding unnecessary repartitioning.

</details>


### [49] [Slasher: Power Flexibility for Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26021)
*Liuzixuan Lin,Fiodar Kazhamiaka,Alok Gautam Kumbhare,Chaojie Zhang,Jaylen Wang,Hassan Khan,Rodrigo L. Assis,Mariana Rodrigues,Kyle Woolcock,Nithish Mahalingam,Brijesh Warrier,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Slasher系统，一个用于Azure数据中心功率调节的通用系统，能够处理从机架级到区域级多数据中心电网事件的各类功率调制场景，通过协调数据中心资源在满足功率目标的同时最小化对托管工作负载的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中心消耗大量电力，经常面临需要调节功率消耗的场景（如基础设施故障、电网故障、电网服务等），这些场景在功率幅度、缩减范围、通知时间等维度上需求各异，需要一个通用的功率调制系统。

Method: 回顾主要功率调制场景，利用生产云数据中心数据表征功率缩减手段，描述Slasher系统架构，形式化云数据中心功率调制控制问题，并开发高保真数据中心模拟器和工作负载影响模型来设计和评估功率控制算法。

Result: 通过高保真模拟器和工作负载影响模型设计了功率控制算法并进行了评估，验证了Slasher系统能够有效协调数据中心资源以实现功率目标。

Conclusion: Slasher为云数据中心提供了一套通用的功率调制解决方案，覆盖从机架到区域级的多种场景，并能最小化对托管工作负载的负面影响。

Abstract: Datacenters consume many megawatts of power, and regularly encounter scenarios that require modulating their power draw. These scenarios include datacenter infrastructure failures, power grid failures, grid services, and more, spanning a diverse range of requirements in terms of the power magnitude, the scope of the reduction, the notice time, and other dimensions. To address these scenarios, we have built Slasher, a general system for modulating the power of \azure datacenters to handle scenarios ranging from individual racks to regional multi-datacenter grid events. Slasher coordinates datacenter resources with the goal of meeting power targets while minimizing negative impact on hosted workloads.
  In this paper, we review the main power modulation scenarios, characterize the power reduction levers using data from production cloud datacenters, describe Slasher's system architecture, and formulate the cloud datacenter power modulation control problem. We also develop a high-fidelity datacenter simulator and propose a workload impact model, using them to design and evaluate power control algorithms.

</details>

### [50] [Learning Continuous Regional Temperature Fields with Lead-Time and Resolution Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25823)
*Chunlei Shi,Jiong Wang,Yi-Lin Wei,Junming Hou,Jinjin Liu,Yecheng Zhang,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时空温度预测器（CSTF），将区域近地表温度（T2M）预测建模为查询条件化的连续时空场评估问题，通过将预测提前时间和输出分辨率作为显式查询，突破了传统深度学习预测器只能输出固定帧和固定网格的限制，在东南中国0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上实现了最优的确定性技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的区域温度预测器通常在预定网格上生成固定集合的未来帧，这限制了当预测产品需要在查询相关的提前时间或显示分辨率下进行评估时的使用场景。

Method: 提出CSTF（连续时空温度预测器），一种神经场方法：首先将多变量ERA5历史数据编码为潜在气象状态，然后将T2M解码为基于坐标的场。空间位置、预测提前时间和输出分辨率作为查询引入，实现统一场评估框架下的标准小时预测、中间提前时间诊断和分辨率可控输出。设计了空间梯度、时间差分和尺度一致性三类正则化目标以保持跨灵活查询的一致性。

Result: 在东南中国0-6小时ERA5-Land基准上，CSTF实现了最优的聚合确定性技能，包括Bias降低17.0%；全局范围的诊断测试进一步展示了灵活的提前时间和分辨率可控推理能力。

Conclusion: 通过将预测提前时间和输出分辨率转化为显式查询，CSTF实现了灵活、连续的温度场评估，为区域短时温度预报提供了一种统一的、超越固定输出约束的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate regional near-surface temperature forecasting is fundamental to short-range weather services and downstream risk assessment. Existing deep learning-based regional forecasters commonly produce a fixed set of future frames on a prescribed grid, limiting their use when forecast products must be evaluated at query-dependent lead times or display resolutions. To overcome these fixed-output constraints, we formulate regional T2M forecasting as query-conditioned continuous spatiotemporal temperature field evaluation and propose the Continuous Spatiotemporal Temperature Forecaster (CSTF), a neural field that turns forecast lead time and output resolution into explicit queries when evaluating 2-m temperature (T2M). Specifically, CSTF first encodes multivariable ERA5 histories into latent meteorological states and then decodes T2M as a coordinate-based field. Accordingly, spatial location, forecast lead time, and output resolution are introduced as queries, enabling standard hourly forecasts, intermediate lead-time diagnostics, and resolution-controllable outputs within a unified field-evaluation framework. Furthermore, to maintain coherence across flexible field queries, we design spatial-gradient, temporal-difference, and scale-consistency objectives that regularize regional thermal structures, lead-wise evolution, and cross-resolution agreement. Experiments on the Southeast China 0-6 h ERA5-Land benchmark demonstrate that CSTF achieves the best aggregate deterministic skill, including a 17.0 percent reduction in Bias, with global-scope diagnostics further illustrating flexible lead-time and resolution-controllable inference.

</details>


### [51] [Dynamic Influence-Weighted Distillation for Single-IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24904)
*Bingxuan Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用训练阶段的四位置IMU信息来提升仅使用右臂IMU的推理模型性能。作者提出动态影响加权（DIW）方法，通过逐样本动态调整知识蒸馏中logit和特征损失的权重，在WEAR数据集上相比固定权重蒸馏提升了6.68个百分点，且推理时无需改变传感器配置或模型结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多位置惯性传感器能提升活动识别精度，但推理阶段部署多个传感器会增加负担。现有方法在训练阶段利用了多传感器信息，但蒸馏时对所有训练样本使用固定权重，忽略了学生模型对不同样本受益程度不同的情况，无法充分利用教师知识。

Method: 采用四IMU冻结教师模型提供logit和特征目标，训练仅使用右臂IMU的学生模型。提出动态影响加权（DIW）：在折叠内训练子集上执行一步候选更新，测试该更新对损失的影响，据此为每个样本的logit损失和特征损失分配独立的逐样本门控权重。

Result: 在WEAR数据集上（19个标签、22名受试者、68,298个完整窗口），使用受试者不相交的五折交叉验证。DIW达到0.638451的池化折叠外宏F1，相比监督学习提升7.66个百分点，相比固定权重知识蒸馏提升6.68个百分点。在19个标签中有18个超过监督学习，22名持出受试者中有21名超越。

Conclusion: DIW方法能将训练阶段的多位置信息有效转化为更强的单IMU模型，推理时保持相同的80,915参数右臂学生模型和相同的传感器部署，无需改变前向图结构。证明了逐样本自适应蒸馏权重捕捉了样本间受益差异，具有实用性。

Abstract: Inertial sensors at multiple body locations can improve activity recognition, but requiring every sensor at inference increases the deployment burden. We study whether four synchronized IMUs available during training can improve a student that uses only the right-arm IMU during fitting and inference. A frozen four-IMU teacher provides logit and feature targets. Fixed-weight knowledge distillation applies each target with the same strength to every fitting sample, although the student may not benefit equally from them. We introduce dynamic influence weighting (DIW), which tests a one-step candidate update on separate fold-internal training participants. DIW then assigns separate sample-wise gates to the logit and feature losses. On WEAR, we evaluate 19 labels and 68,298 complete windows from 22 participants using subject-disjoint five-fold cross-validation. Pooled out-of-fold macro-F1 is 0.561820 for Supervised and 0.571623 for Fixed-weight KD. DIW reaches 0.638451, gains of 7.66 and 6.68 percentage points, respectively. It exceeds Supervised for 18 of 19 labels and 21 of 22 held-out participants. All three routes retain the same 80,915-parameter right-arm student at inference. Under this protocol, DIW converts training-only multi-position information into a stronger single-IMU model without changing deployed sensing or the student forward graph.

</details>


### [52] [GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny: A Compact Embedding Model for Legal Domain Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24936)
*Surya Saka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny，一个0.6B参数的法律领域嵌入检索模型。该模型在MLEB基准上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B以下参数模型中表现优异。通过两阶段训练（知识蒸馏+领域微调）结合340万查询-段落对数据（含15万人工标注样本）以及多级量化推理架构，实现了资源受限环境下的高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域检索需要专门化的嵌入模型，但现有通用模型在专业领域表现不佳，且大型模型难以在资源受限环境中部署，如何在紧凑模型上实现专业领域的检索性能是核心挑战。

Method: 采用两阶段训练流程：第一阶段从大型教师模型蒸馏知识到紧凑的学生架构；第二阶段结合硬负样本挖掘进行领域特定微调。使用包含340万查询-段落对（其中15万为跨司法管辖区的人工标注样本）的精心策划数据集，并设计了支持BF16、INT8和二值等多种量化级别的推理架构。

Result: GreenLeaf-Tiny在MLEB上达到75.11%，在MTEB(Law, v1)上达到64.38%，在1B参数以下模型中展现出竞争性性能，同时通过多级量化支持实现了资源受限环境下的高效部署。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合高质量数据的领域特定训练可以显著提升专业领域嵌入模型性能，即使在紧凑模型规模下也能获得有竞争力的检索结果，为法律及其他专业领域的轻量级模型部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We present GreenLeaf Law Embed Tiny, a 0.6B parameter embedding model for legal domain retrieval. GreenLeaf-Tiny achieves 75.11% on the Massive Legal Embedding Benchmark (MLEB) and 64.38% on MTEB(Law, v1),demonstrating competitive performance among models under 1B parameters. Our approach combines a two-stage training pipeline that first distills knowledge from a larger teacher model into a compact student architecture, then applies domain-specific fine-tuning with hard negative mining; a carefully curated dataset of 3.4 million query-passage pairs, including 150,000 human-curated samples across diverse legal jurisdictions; and an efficient inference architecture supporting multiple quantization levels (BF16, INT8, binary) enabling deployment in resource-constrained environments. We provide detailed analysis of our training methodology, architectural choices, and comprehensive evaluation across legal retrieval tasks. Our results demonstrate that domain-specific training with high-quality data can improve performance for specialized domain applications

</details>


### [53] [Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24937)
*Xudong Mou,Zexin Wu,Chuan Luo,Shiru Chen,Xudong Liu,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从假设驱动的视角对多模态异常检测（MMAD）领域进行了系统性综述，将现有方法分为正态性假设和异常性假设两大范式，并探讨了基础模型对该领域的重塑作用及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMAD在工业检测和网络安全等安全关键应用中日益重要，但现有文献在不同领域和模态组合间分散割裂，且已有综述多按架构而非异常定义与分离方式来组织方法，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 本文采用假设驱动的综述框架：首先形式化MMAD问题，识别其核心挑战背后的五个内在特征；然后将现有工作分为正态性假设方法（通过表征学习、跨模态对齐和知识增强建模规律性）和异常性假设方法（通过粗粒度、结构化和语义异常注入锐化决策边界）两大互补范式；最后调研基础模型对MMAD的影响。

Result: 整理了两大互补范式下的方法体系，汇总了跨领域的代表性基准和评估协议，并分析了基础模型通过可扩展预训练、灵活跨模态迁移和新兴推理能力对MMAD的变革性影响。

Conclusion: MMAD领域仍需发展鲁棒、自适应、可解释的系统，开放问题包括基础模型的深度融合以及如何构建更统一的评估体系等未来研究方向。

Abstract: Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection (MMAD) detects rare abnormal events from heterogeneous data sources and is increasingly used in safety- and reliability-critical applications such as industrial inspection and cybersecurity. Yet the literature is fragmented across domains and modality combinations, and existing surveys usually group methods by architecture rather than by how abnormality is defined and separated in multi-modal settings. We survey MMAD from an assumption-driven perspective. We formalize the problem, identify five intrinsic characteristics underlying its core challenges, and organize prior work into two complementary paradigms. The first, normality-assumption methods, models regularity via representation learning, cross-modal alignment, and knowledge enhancement. The second, anomaly-assumption methods, sharpens decision boundaries through coarse-grained, structural, and semantic anomaly injection. We also investigate how foundation models are reshaping MMAD through scalable pretraining, flexible cross-modal transfer, and emerging reasoning capabilities. Finally, we compile representative benchmarks and evaluation protocols across domains and highlight open problems and future directions for robust, adaptive, and interpretable MMAD systems.

</details>


### [54] [ExFold: Unified Expert Folding for Training-Free MoE Prefill-Decode Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24938)
*Juntong Wu,Yifei Liu,Junyi Chen,Siqi Fan,Chaoran Feng,Minghao Li,Liujie Zhang,Weihang Chen,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExFold是一个统一的免训练专家折叠框架，通过将预算外的专家贡献投影到保留专家上，同时加速MoE模型的prefill和decode阶段，在vLLM中实现最高1.41倍TTFT和2.45倍TPOT加速，同时保持约99%的原始质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然通过稀疏专家激活保持每token计算量有界，但其低延迟服务面临prefill和decode两个瓶颈不同的推理阶段（prefill受token级专家计算主导，decode受batch级激活专家集的内存流量约束）。现有免训练加速方法只优化单一资源代理，且要么丢弃被排除专家的贡献，要么隐含近似，缺乏统一的联合优化方案。

Method: ExFold将prefill和decode统一建模为预算化的输出近似问题：仅执行阶段特定的受限专家集，同时通过校准的标量投影器将预算外专家的贡献折叠到保留专家上。其核心是基于"许多专家输出方向对齐但幅度不同"的观察，在无标签数据上校准成对标量投影矩阵，推理时用于折叠。prefill加速为token级Top-K折叠，decode加速为batch级专家池折叠，两阶段共享同一折叠机制，仅在保留专家的选择方式上有所不同。

Result: 在vLLM中实现为即插即用插件，配备轻量级专家折叠CUDA内核，实现最高1.41倍TTFT（首token延迟）和2.45倍TPOT（每个输出token延迟）加速，同时保持约99%的原始平均质量。

Conclusion: ExFold证明了通过统一的专家折叠机制可以同时解决MoE推理中prefill和decode两个阶段的差异化瓶颈，无需训练即可在显著加速的同时保持高质量，为MoE高效低延迟服务提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity for strong quality while keeping per-token compute bounded through sparse expert activation. Yet low-latency MoE serving is increasingly challenging, because it spans two inference phases with fundamentally different bottlenecks: prefill is dominated by token-wise expert computation, whereas decode is constrained by memory traffic from the batch-wise activated expert set. However, existing training-free acceleration methods optimize only a single resource proxy, either the experts each token executes or the experts a batch activates, and either discard the excluded experts' contribution or leave it only implicitly approximated. In this paper, we propose ExFold, a unified training-free expert-folding framework for jointly accelerating MoE prefill and decode. ExFold casts both prefill and decode as one budgeted output-approximation problem: execute only a phase-specific constrained expert set while projecting the contribution of budget-excluded experts onto retained experts using calibrated scalar projectors. Motivated by the observation that many expert outputs are directionally aligned but differ in magnitude, ExFold calibrates a pairwise scalar-projector matrix on unlabeled data and uses it at inference time to fold excluded expert contributions into retained experts. Under this view, prefill acceleration becomes token-level Top-K folding, and decode acceleration becomes batch-level expert-pool folding. The two phases differ only in how retained experts are selected, while excluded contributions are recovered by one shared folding mechanism. We implement ExFold as a plug-and-play plugin in vLLM, with a lightweight expert-folding CUDA kernel, delivering up to 1.41x TTFT and 2.45x TPOT speedups while retaining about 99% of the original average quality.

</details>


### [55] [When Does Frequency Decomposition Benefit Physics-Informed Neural Networks? A Preliminary Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24940)
*Shubham Rai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双分支频谱门控架构（DBSG-PINN），将低频和高频成分分离到独立子网络中，并通过自适应门控连接。在5个一维基准PDE上的部分受控消融实验表明，频率分解在频谱复杂的基准上效果显著（最高降低L2误差59.2%），但在较平滑的PDE上收益甚微，门控机制的收益与目标解的频谱丰富程度成正比。由于仅使用单一训练种子，本文定位为探索性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PINN技术（如傅里叶特征嵌入、正弦激活函数）假设频谱处理方法普遍有效，但缺乏对不同频谱状态下实际收益的验证。作者旨在揭示频率分解和频谱路由在不同频谱复杂度PDE上的差异化表现，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出双分支频谱门控架构（DBSG-PINN），将低频与高频分量分别路由至两个子网络，通过自适应门控单元融合。通过部分受控消融实验，分离研究频率分解和频谱路由两个因素的效果，并在5个一维基准PDE上（从平滑单尺度到振荡多尺度）进行测试。

Result: 频率分解在频谱复杂的基准上效果显著，在模态波问题上将相对L2误差降低最多59.2%；在较平滑的PDE上收益甚微。在1D Wave基准上，完整模型表现明显差于更简单的固定组合变体。门控的收益与目标解的频谱丰富程度呈正相关，在多尺度基准上优势最大，在单尺度上优势最小或为负。

Conclusion: 频谱门控的有效性依赖于目标解的光谱结构，并非普适性方法。该研究为探索性研究，基于单一训练种子，需要更多种子和基准来验证结论的稳健性，引发了对频谱路由策略在不同场景下适用性的思考。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) often have high-frequency and multi-scale features that neural networks struggle to approximate. Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) build the governing equations directly into training, but suffer from spectral bias: they learn low-frequency components faster than high-frequency ones. Techniques such as Fourier feature embeddings and sinusoidal activations address this, but most studies assume they help across the board without checking which spectral regimes actually benefit. We introduce a dual-branch, spectrally-gated architecture (DBSG-PINN) that splits low- and high-frequency components into separate subnetworks joined by an adaptive gate, and use it to run a partially controlled ablation of frequency decomposition and spectral routing. We test this on five one-dimensional benchmark PDEs, ranging from smooth, single-scale problems to oscillatory, multi-scale ones. Frequency decomposition helps most on the spectrally complex benchmarks, cutting relative $L_2$ error by up to $59.2\%$ on a multimodal wave problem, but gives little benefit on smoother PDEs. On one benchmark (1D Wave), it performs substantially worse than a simpler fixed-combination variant. The gate's benefit scales with how spectrally rich the target solution is: the full model's advantage over the ablations is largest on multi-scale benchmarks and smallest (or negative) on single-scale ones, consistent with the gate exploiting frequency structure rather than acting as noise,though we do not directly visualize or quantify its spatial activations in this study. All results come from a single training seed across five 1D benchmarks, so we present this as an exploratory study meant to raise questions rather than answer them, and outline the additional seeds and benchmarks needed to test whether the pattern holds.

</details>


### [56] [FAMPWQ: Fisher Information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization for Effective LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24945)
*Gongwei Lee,Ji Liu,Juncheng Jia,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息量的自适应混合精度权重量化方法FAMPWQ，通过层自适应量化实现大语言模型在资源受限设备上的高效推理，在7个模型和5个基准上显著优于7个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型资源消耗巨大，难以部署在资源受限设备上。传统量化方法因采用均匀位宽或简单启发式敏感度评估，通常会导致严重的性能退化。

Method: 首先提出一种基于Fisher信息量的系统模型，用于衡量各层对量化的敏感程度；随后设计基于强化学习的位宽分配器，根据Fisher信息敏感度指标生成自适应的位宽分配策略。

Result: 在7个模型和5个基准上的大量实验表明，FAMPWQ在PPL（最多降低3.39）、准确率（最多提升6.87%）以及LLM-as-a-judge对比（最高76%胜率）方面均显著优于7个基线方法。

Conclusion: FAMPWQ通过Fisher信息量驱动的层自适应混合精度量化，能够有效提升LLM在资源受限设备上的推理性能，证明了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed remarkable achievements of Large Language Models (LLMs) in multiple domains, while the excessive resource requirements of LLMs hinder the deployment on resource-constrained devices. Although model quantization stands out as an effective approach, conventional quantization approaches typically incur severe performance degradation due to uniform bit-width or simple heuristic sensitivity evaluation. In this paper, we propose a novel Fisher information-based Adaptive Mixed Precision Weight Quantization approach, i.e., FAMPWQ, which performs layer-adaptive weight quantization for effective LLM inference on commodity GPUs. First, we propose a system model with a novel Fisher information metric to measure the layer-wise sensitivity to quantization. Second, we propose a reinforcement learning-based bit-width allocator in FAMPWQ, which generates an adaptive bit-width allocation strategy based on the Fisher information sensitivity metric. Extensive experiments on 7 models and 5 benchmarks demonstrate that FAMPWQ significantly outperforms 7 baseline approaches in terms of PPL (up to 3.39 smaller), accuracy (up to 6.87% higher), and LLM-as-a-judge comparison (up to 76% win rate).

</details>


### [57] [MacroAgent: Regularity-Aware Macro Legalization with LLM-Agent-Designed Contour Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24946)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Xufeng Yao,Yuxuan Zhao,Yuntao Lu,Peiyu Liao,Zuodong Zhang,Yibo Lin,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MacroAgent，一个基于大语言模型（LLM）驱动的四阶段宏单元合法化框架，通过聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配和簇间优化，显著提升布局规则性和布线质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有宏单元合法化方法存在鲁棒性不足、计算成本高或忽视宏单元间规则性等问题，而宏单元位置对VLSI设计最终质量影响重大。

Method: 提出四阶段框架：聚类、轮廓生成、模板匹配和簇间精化。利用大语言模型发现多种有效的启发式规则感知轮廓算法，生成鲁棒且有效的宏单元合法化算法方案。

Result: 在TILOS和Chipyard基准上，布局规则性提升2至8倍，布线线长减少3%至5%，拥塞情况相当，鲁棒性显著改善且运行时间可接受。端到端评估中，路由线长降低2.9%，TNS改善68.3%。

Conclusion: MacroAgent通过LLM驱动的启发式算法发现，在宏单元合法化任务中实现了规则性、线长和PPA指标的全方位改进，验证了LLM在EDA自动化设计中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Macros constitute a large part of the core area in modern very large-scale integration (VLSI) designs. Moreover, macro positions have a significant impact on the final quality of result (QoR), and macro legalization is typically the final step in determining the macro positions. However, existing approaches related to macro legalization either lack robustness or incur substantial computational costs or neglect the regularity between macros. To address these limitations, we introduce MacroAgent. The novel framework is a four-stage approach: clustering, contour generation, template matching, and inter-cluster refinement. We propose leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) to discover multiple, effective heuristic regularity-aware contour algorithms. This framework successfully generates robust and effective algorithmic solutions for macro legalization. Compared with state-of-the-art macro legalization works, experimental results on TILOS and Chipyard benchmarks demonstrate a 2 to 8 fold improvement in layout regularity, a 3% to 5% reduction in routed wirelength with comparable congestion after global routing, and significantly better robustness with an acceptable runtime. Furthermore, end-to-end evaluation through Cadence Innovus place-and-route confirms that the regularity improvements translate into tangible PPA gains, including 2.9% lower routed wirelength and 68.3% TNS improvement over the DREAMPlace macro legalization baseline; it also achieves 1.8% lower routed wirelength when integrated into the Innovus macro placement flow.

</details>


### [58] [CAT-GS: Balanced Multimodal Learning via Calibrated Gating and Fusion Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24947)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Sharjil Khan,Md. Samiul Alim,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAT-GS是一个针对多模态神经网络训练不稳定问题的优化控制器，通过温度缩放和EMA平滑校准教师置信度，采用边际阈值策略切换三种模态训练模式，并通过梯度预算重归一化和融合层PCGrad来稳定训练动力学，在多个音视频及跨域基准上优于或持平强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端多模态神经网络训练常出现三种耦合的失效模式：模态不平衡（单模态主导梯度优化）、不稳定门控（置信度噪声导致模态选择混乱）和融合干扰（融合层梯度冲突）。这些问题严重影响多模态网络的训练稳定性和最终性能。

Method: CAT-GS在反向传播阶段工作，不修改模型结构、融合模块或任务损失。具体包括：(1) 通过温度缩放和EMA平滑校准教师可靠性；(2) 采用边际阈值策略在warm-up dropout、弱模态优先和弱偏置混合三种模式间切换；(3) 通过封顶梯度预算重归一化稳定激进门控下的梯度幅度；(4) 在融合层使用PCGrad减少跨模态干扰。

Result: 在CREMA-D、AV-MNIST、VGGSound、UR-FUNNY等多模态基准及AVE、CMU-MOSI等跨域数据集上，CAT-GS在融合多模态准确率上优于或持平OGM-GE、G2D、UMT等强基线方法，并展现出更平滑的门控行为和更少的融合梯度冲突。

Conclusion: CAT-GS通过校准的、自适应的、阈值化的门控策略和融合手术技术，有效缓解了多模态训练中的模态不平衡、不稳定门控和融合干扰问题，在多个基准上验证了其作为神经动力学优化控制器的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: End-to-end training of multimodal neural networks often exhibits unstable neural dynamics characterized by three coupled failure modes that degrade learning: (i) modality imbalance, where one branch dominates gradient-based optimization; (ii) unstable gating, where noisy confidence cues induce erratic modality selection; and (iii) fusion interference, where modality-specific gradients conflict at the shared fusion layer. We propose CAT-GS (Calibrated, Adaptive, Thresholded Gating with Fusion Surgery), a neural dynamics-based optimization controller for intelligent computing applications. CAT-GS operates during backpropagation without modifying model architectures, fusion modules, or task losses. Through calibration of teacher-derived reliability via temperature scaling and EMA smoothing, CAT-GS stabilizes neural dynamics using a margin-thresholded policy to switch between warm-up dropout, weak-modality prioritization, and weak-biased blending, stabilizes gradient magnitudes under aggressive gating via capped gradient-budget renormalization, and applies fusion-only PCGrad to reduce destructive cross-modal interference at the primary shared bottleneck. We evaluate CAT-GS on audio--visual multimodal pattern recognition benchmarks (CREMA-D, AV-MNIST, and VGGSound), a tri-modal setting (UR-FUNNY), controlled synthetic data (CG-MNIST), and additional cross-domain benchmarks (AVE and CMU-MOSI). CAT-GS improves or matches fused multimodal accuracy against strong imbalance-aware baselines (including OGM-GE, G$^2$D, and UMT) across settings, and yields smoother gating behavior with fewer conflicting fusion gradients.

</details>


### [59] [Demystifying Reinforcement Learning Post-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24949)
*Donovan Clay,Saket Gollapudi,Sankar Harilal,Min Jang,Jacob Morrison,Sewoong Oh,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统解构了大语言模型（LLM）的强化学习（RL）后训练算法，在受控简化环境中逐一剖析各步骤，揭示了基础模型先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模如何影响RL后训练效果，并通过策略输出分布的熵作为透镜，比较预训练、SFT和RL后训练各阶段对模型确定性的塑造作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RL后训练已成为提升LLM推理、数学和编码能力的关键框架，但对许多研究者和从业者而言，经典RL背后的原理仍然是个"黑箱"。作者旨在揭开这一黑箱，为NLP社区提供一份关于RL后训练的内部运作机制的清晰科普资源。

Method: 在受控且简化的环境中隔离"可验证奖励的RL"（RL with Verifiable Rewards）的各个机制步骤，分别考察基础模型先验分布、奖励信号粒度、提示分布多样性和模型规模等关键因素对RL结果的影响，并以策略输出分布的熵作为度量工具，对比预训练、SFT和RL后训练三个阶段学习的分布差异。

Result: 研究发现这些因素的选择相互作用并影响后训练效果。例如，"伪奖励"（spurious rewards）的影响取决于后训练所使用的提示分布；RL后训练的成功与否取决于基础模型是否已对期望行为分配了足够的概率质量，这与经典RL中的探索概念相关联。

Conclusion: 本文作为一份入门资料（primer），为NLP社区中希望将RL纳入工具箱的研究者和实践者提供了对RL后训练内部机制的系统性理解，澄清了各步骤之间的相互作用及其对最终训练效果的影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing the capabilities of large language models (LLMs), enabling impressive reasoning, math, and coding capabilities. Yet for many researchers and practitioners, the principles behind classical RL remain a "black box". In this work, we deconstruct the RL post-training algorithm, investigating each step to clarify what is actually happening beneath the surface. By isolating the mechanics of RL with Verifiable Rewards in a controlled and simplified environment, we examine how RL outcomes are shaped by the base model's prior distribution, the granularity of the reward signal, the diversity of the prompt distribution, and model scale. We use the entropy of the policy's output distribution as a lens to compare the distributions learned through pretraining, SFT, and RL post-training, revealing how each stage shapes model certainty. Our investigation sheds light on how these choices interact to affect post-training success. For example, we show that the effect of so-called 'spurious rewards' depends on the prompt distribution used for post-training. We also provide insight into why the success of RL post-training depends on whether the base model already places sufficient probability mass on the desired behavior, linking it to the classical concept of exploration in RL. Ultimately, we provide this primer as a resource to those in the NLP community wishing to incorporate RL as a tool in their toolbox.

</details>


### [60] [Resilient Decentralized Wireless Federated Learning via Gradient Tracking with AdamW](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25535)
*Nguyen Van Thieu,Ti Ti Nguyen,Ons Aouedi,Vu Nguyen Ha,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEF-GT-AdamW算法，一种面向无线物联网边缘网络的通信高效且抗中断的分布式学习算法，该算法结合梯度跟踪、AdamW自适应优化、双流有偏量化与误差反馈机制，并采用本地回退策略应对不可靠的广播通信，在异构数据和通信受限的无线链路上实现了更优的鲁棒性和收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线IoT边缘网络需要能够在异构本地数据和通信受限链路下可靠运行的分布式学习方法，但现有分散式优化方案在严格的空中时间预算、衰落信道和丢包条件下往往产生巨大的通信开销和性能退化。

Method: QEF-GT-AdamW算法结合三方面技术：梯度跟踪缓解非IID数据的影响；AdamW自适应优化提升训练稳定性；双流有偏量化与误差反馈减少模型和跟踪交换的通信负载。同时采用本地回退策略处理不可靠的广播通信，并显式建模带宽、发射功率、空中时间约束和衰落信道对分布式学习性能的影响。

Result: 在异构MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上的实验表明，QEF-GT-AdamW在鲁棒性和收敛性能上持续优于代表性分布式学习基线方法，并在有限无线资源下实现了良好的精度-通信权衡。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在压缩且不可靠的无线通信条件下，通过合理的算法设计（梯度跟踪+自适应优化+量化压缩）可以同时保证收敛性能并显著降低通信开销，为无线边缘网络中的分布式学习提供了实用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Wireless Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge networks require decentralized learning (DecL) methods that can operate reliably under both heterogeneous local data and communication-constrained wireless links. However, existing decentralized optimization schemes often incur substantial communication overhead and degraded performance when transmissions are constrained by strict airtime budgets, fading channels, and packet losses. This paper proposes QEF-GT-AdamW, a communication-efficient and outage-resilient algorithm for DecL over wireless communication (WCom) networks. The proposed method combines gradient tracking to mitigate the effect of non-IID data, AdamW-based adaptive optimization to improve training stability, and dual-stream biased quantization with error feedback to reduce communication payloads for both model and tracking exchanges. To address unreliable broadcast communication, the proposed framework further employs a local fallback strategy when scheduled packets are not successfully received. We explicitly model the effect of bandwidth, transmit power, airtime constraints, and fading channels on DecL performance, and establish convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm under compressed and unreliable wireless communication. Experimental results on heterogeneous MNIST and CIFAR-10 settings show that QEF-GT-AdamW consistently improves robustness and convergence performance over representative DecL baselines while achieving favorable accuracy-communication trade-offs under limited wireless resources.

</details>


### [61] [AFDBench: A Reasoning-First AI Scientist for NationalWeather Service Forecast Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24954)
*Manmeet Singh,Somnath Luitel,Prabhjot Singh,Manraaj Banga,Naveen Sudharsan,Josh Durkee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AFDBench，这是首个评估生成式气象推理的基准，包含7,732篇专家撰写的区域预报讨论及三个互补指标（Met-Align、Style-Align、Input-Grounding）。通过GRPO强化学习，7B参数模型在未见过的NWS办公室样本上将Style-Align从0.318提升至0.619，Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成高风险的气象文本时会产生数值幻觉，这对气象传播构成风险，需要专门的基准来评估和改善LLM在气象领域的生成能力。

Method: 构建AFDBench基准，包含来自13个NWS办公室的7,732篇专家撰写的AFD文本及配对的真实AI天气预报数据（Google WeatherNext 2），设计三个评估指标（Met-Align数值准确性、Style-Align专业术语规范性、Input-Grounding数据忠实度），并应用GRPO强化学习配以领域特定奖励来训练模型。

Result: 零样本评估显示开源LLM的Style-Align较低（~0.33），Input-Grounding中等（~0.88）。经GRPO训练后，在1,033个未见过的样本上，Style-Align从0.318提升至0.619（近乎翻倍），Input-Grounding从0.881提升至0.940。

Conclusion: 结果表明强化学习能够教会7B参数模型像专业气象学家一样写作，并忠实解读AI气象数据，有效缓解数值幻觉问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) hallucinate numerical values when generating high-stakes meteorological text, posing risks for weather communication. We present AFDBench, an AI meteorologist that generates professional Area Forecast Discussions (AFDs) by reasoning through structured AI weather forecast data from Google's WeatherNext 2. We introduce AFDBench, the first benchmark for evaluating generative meteorological reasoning, comprising 7,732 expert written discussions from 13 National Weather Service (NWS) offices paired with real AI weather forecast inputs, and three complementary metrics: Met-Align (numerical accuracy), Style-Align (professional dialect adherence), and Input-Grounding (fidelity to source weather data). Zero-shot evaluations reveal that open-source LLMs achieve low Style-Align (~0.33) and moderate Input-Grounding (~0.88), failing to write in the professional NWS register or faithfully use their input data. We apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with domain-specific rewards targeting temperature accuracy, synoptic correctness, and format compliance. On 1,033 held-out samples from two unseen NWS offices, GRPO nearly doubles Style-Align from 0.318 to 0.619 and improves Input-Grounding from 0.881 to 0.940, demonstrating that reinforcement learning teaches a 7B-parameter model to write like a professional meteorologist and faithfully interpret AI weather data.

</details>


### [62] [Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Aggregation in Semi-Supervised Federated Learning with non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25794)
*Rene Glitza,Luca Becker,Rainer Martin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出pFedMARL，一种利用多智能体强化学习（MARL）与TD3算法动态调整联邦学习聚合策略的方法，通过服务器端智能体调整客户端贡献、客户端智能体平衡全局与本地更新，在非IID数据分布和对抗性客户端场景下优于FedAvg、Ditto等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临客户端数据异构（非IID）分布带来的全局模型偏差和次优问题，现有聚合方法难以自适应地应对这种异质性。

Method: 提出pFedMARL框架：服务器端使用TD3智能体动态调整各客户端的聚合权重（贡献），客户端使用智能体平衡全局模型与本地个性化更新，无需预训练，通过多智能体强化学习实现自适应聚合策略优化。

Result: 在半监督音频频谱变换器（audio spectrogram transformer）的训练中，pFedMARL在多种非IID场景和存在对抗性客户端的情况下匹配或超越了FedAvg、Ditto和本地训练方法。

Conclusion: pFedMARL能有效提升联邦学习的准确性、鲁棒性和公平性，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed training of machine learning models while preserving data privacy. However, FL struggles with heterogeneous, non-IID client data distributions, resulting in sub-optimal and biased global models. In this paper, we propose pFedMARL, a novel approach leveraging Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) with Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to dynamically adapt aggregation strategies in FL settings. Our method employs a server-side agent adjusting client contributions to optimize global model robustness and client-side agents balancing global and local updates to personalize models effectively without pre-training. We demonstrate superior performance of pFedMARL for training a semi-supervised audio spectrogram transformer, matching or outperforming FedAvg, Ditto, and local training approaches across multiple non-IID scenarios and in the presence of adversarial clients. Our results indicate that pFedMARL actively improves accuracy, robustness, and fairness, making it suitable for real-world deployments.

</details>


### [63] [Why and When Neural Networks Improve Local Approximation in Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24963)
*Chengkuo Bian,Pengcheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了神经代理模型在无导数优化中效果矛盾的根本原因，指出三个决定性因素——角色（Role）、半径（Radius）和空间（Room）——而非训练精度，决定了学习到的局部模型能否带来收益。通过形式化半径感知的局部泛化概念，并在117个基准实例及多个求解器上进行系统验证，证明了正确的代理使用方式可以提升求解性能，而错误的使用方式则会造成损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中神经代理模型在无导数优化中的应用效果相互矛盾：同一类模型在某些求解器中能显著减少评估次数，在另一些求解器中却毫无作用甚至使性能变差。这种矛盾阻碍了神经代理模型的可靠部署，本文旨在揭示矛盾的根源并建立可预测收益的理论框架。

Method: 作者识别并形式化三个决定代理模型价值的因素：(1) 角色——代理模型仅提议候选解（由真实目标函数审批）有帮助，而替代求解器所依赖的梯度则有损害；(2) 半径——拟合于优化路径的模型仅在有限邻域内可靠，其误差既不随邻域缩小而消失，也无法在邻域扩大后保持；(3) 空间——代理模型只能加速基础方法本身仍能实现的进步。作者建立了半径感知的局部泛化形式化定义，并将其与经典的完全线性条件联系起来，在固定代理类、训练流程和基础方法的条件下系统测试每个因素。

Result: 在117个基准实例上，受保护的辅助策略将高精度求解实例数从67提升到84，而梯度替换使其降至65；从训练损失中移除梯度项使代理接受率从0.703降至0.148；在10个噪声水平上的1000次配对比较表明不存在噪声阈值，只有提前停止的基础方法才受影响。模型信任域求解器在附加相同代理后从88降至86（空间不足），发布的插值软件保持领先（103）。在蒙特卡洛库存模型中，修复接受接口价值10.40成本单位，而代理本身价值为0。

Conclusion: 神经代理模型在无导数优化中的价值取决于角色、半径和空间三个因素，而非训练精度。只有以被保护的角色（提出候选而非替代梯度）、在可信半径内使用、并为基础方法保留改进空间时，代理模型才值得部署。

Abstract: Published experience with neural surrogates in derivative-free optimisation is contradictory: the same family of models that cuts the evaluation count of one solver leaves another unchanged, or makes it worse. We show that the contradiction dissolves once three factors are stated, and that these, rather than the fit accuracy a training curve reports, are what delimit when a learned local model pays. Role: a surrogate that proposes candidates the true objective must still approve helps, while one that replaces a gradient the solver depends on hurts. Radius: a model fitted to an optimisation path is reliable only inside a bounded neighbourhood, and its error neither vanishes as that neighbourhood shrinks nor survives its growth. Room: a surrogate can only accelerate progress the base method is still able to make. We formalise radius-aware local generalisation, relate it to the classical fully linear condition, and test each factor with the surrogate class, training pipeline and base method held fixed. Over 117 benchmark instances safeguarded assistance raises the instances solved to high accuracy from 67 to 84 while gradient replacement lowers them to 65; removing the gradient term from the training loss cuts surrogate acceptance from 0.703 to 0.148; and 1000 paired comparisons over ten noise levels show no noise threshold, only a base method that stops early. The same factors bound the gain: a model-based trust-region solver, which leaves little room, drops from 88 to 86 when the identical surrogate is attached, and released interpolation software stays ahead at 103, and on a Monte-Carlo inventory model repairing the acceptance interface is worth 10.40 cost units against 0.00 for the surrogate.

</details>


### [64] [Physics-Informed Error Field Learning: A Post-Training Optimization Framework for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24970)
*Jiuyun Sun,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为PIEFL的框架，通过引入辅助误差网络来学习当前近似解与精确解之间的误差场，从而替代对原始网络的持续优化，在相同计算预算下提升PINNs的求解精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在优化后期，参数更新带来的精度提升逐渐减少，但计算成本却持续增加，存在显著的效率瓶颈。传统方法始终让单个网络逼近解场，未能有效利用计算资源。

Method: PIEFL框架在主网络达到满意精度后，引入辅助误差网络。通过推导物理约束下的误差控制方程，让误差网络学习当前近似与精确解之间的差距，并将学习到的误差修正与主网络预测相结合以提高精度。该框架无需修改主网络架构。

Result: 在代表性PDE上的数值实验表明，PIEFL在相同计算预算下实现了更高的求解精度，验证了其在提升PINNs性能方面的有效性。

Conclusion: PIEFL是一种通用的事后优化策略，兼容现有PINN模型，通过将学习目标从解场转向误差场来避免持续优化整个解空间，从而更高效地利用计算资源提升精度。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have emerged as an important class of numerical methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). However, during the late-stage optimization process, further parameter updates often yield diminishing accuracy improvements while increasing computational costs. To address this issue, this paper proposes a Physics-Informed Error Field Learning (PIEFL) framework for PINNs. Unlike conventional approaches that continuously approximate the solution field using a single network, PIEFL introduces an auxiliary error network after the primary network achieves satisfactory accuracy and shifts the learning objective from the solution field to the error field. By deriving error control equations under physical constraints, the error network learns the discrepancy between the current approximation and the exact solution, and the learned error correction is combined with the primary prediction to improve solution accuracy. The proposed framework avoids continuous optimization of the entire solution space and focuses computational resources on correcting existing prediction errors. Moreover, PIEFL requires no modification to the primary network architecture, making it compatible with existing PINN models and applicable as a general post-training optimization strategy. Numerical experiments on representative PDEs demonstrate that PIEFL achieves higher solution accuracy under the same computational budget, validating its effectiveness in improving the performance of PINNs.

</details>


### [65] [Resource-Efficient Pruning for Transformer via Low-Rank Importance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24973)
*Peng Liu,Huibing Zeng,Yiqun Zhang,Yang Yi,Jigang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REP-LIE，一种在微调过程中进行资源高效剪枝的新方法，利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度估计权重重要性，无需全梯度计算，并通过稳定性评分迭代剪枝不重要的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Transformer模型计算和内存开销巨大，传统剪枝方法依赖全梯度重要性估计且需要预先微调模型，资源消耗难以接受。

Method: 利用LoRA低秩矩阵的梯度估算权重重要性，引入稳定性评分解决重要性估计的随机性问题，基于该评分迭代剪枝不重要参数，并通过轻量级更新微调剪枝后的模型。

Result: 在中型编码器模型和大型生成模型（LLaMA-7B、Mistral-7B）上的广泛实验表明，REP-LIE相比现有方法仍能达到有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: REP-LIE能够在微调过程中实现资源高效的剪枝，显著降低计算开销的同时保持模型性能，为资源受限环境下的模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of large-scale pre-trained language models based on Transformer architectures, their high computational and memory costs have become a major obstacle to deployment, especially in resource-constrained environments. Traditional pruning methods typically depend on full gradient-based importance estimation, and they necessitate prior finetuning of the model to achieve satisfactory performance. This process often results in intolerable resource consumption. This paper proposes REP-LIE, a new approach to enable resource-efficient pruning during the process of finetuning. REP-LIE leverages the gradients of LoRA low-rank matrices to estimate the importance of weights without requiring full gradient computation. To address the inherent randomness in importance estimation, a stability score is introduced, serving as the basis for iterative pruning of unimportant model parameters. The pruned model is further finetuned through lightweight updates, eliminating the need for full-parameter optimization in the process of finetuning. Extensive experiments on both medium-scale encoder models and large-scale generative models (LLaMA-7B and Mistral-7B) demonstrate that REP-LIE still achieves competitive performance compared to existing approaches.

</details>


### [66] [A Storage-Retrieval Gap in Parametric Knowledge Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25489)
*Martino M. L. Pulici,Cuong Xuan Chu,Evgeny Kharlamov,Volker Tresp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究将知识图谱编译为基于实体的LoRA适配器库作为参数化知识层的方案，替代传统将子图注入上下文的图检索增强生成方法。实验表明适配器能编码事实知识且正确适配器可恢复知识（oracle gap +0.283），但基于语义相似度的检索方法（嵌入与权重几何）均无法有效找回知识，存在"知识本地存储不迁移"的核心问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图检索增强生成（Graph RAG）每次查询都将子图注入上下文，产生重复的token成本和数据暴露风险。作者希望探索一种替代方案：离线将知识图谱编译为LoRA适配器库，通过注入权重而非文本进行查询，实现查询时零上下文成本，避免数据泄露。

Method: 将知识图谱离线编译为每个实体对应的LoRA适配器库作为参数化知识层。在MetaQA数据集上，对每个实体用其子图训练适配器，查询时通过注入正确适配器权重而非文本进行回答。对比了闭卷基座模型、子图训练适配器、基于嵌入和权重几何的检索方法的效果。

Result: 在MetaQA上：单值关系上适配器相对于闭卷基座（0.007近乎盲）提升+0.243精确匹配分数；仅正确的适配器能恢复知识（oracle gap +0.283）。但基于嵌入相似度和权重几何的检索均败于随机水平，因为语义相邻实体的适配器不包含答案——知识本地存储不迁移。权重几何与子图语义相关（ρ=+0.329）但与功能可检索性无关。

Conclusion: 参数化知识图谱记忆在知识存储层面是可行的，但核心开放问题在于如何通过语义相似度之外的机制选择和组合正确的适配器，这需要学习得到的、查询条件化的组合机制来解决。

Abstract: Graph retrieval-augmented generation places retrieved subgraphs into the model's context window at query time, paying a recurring token cost and exposing source data on every call. We study an alternative: compiling a knowledge graph offline into a bank of LoRA adapters, one per entity, that serve as a parametric knowledge layer queried by injecting weights rather than text, at zero query-time context cost. On the MetaQA dataset, we find that subgraph-trained adapters encode context-free factual knowledge that generalizes to unseen questions: on single-valued relations the adapter gains $+0.243$ exact-match score over a base model that is nearly blind closed-book ($0.007$), and only the correct adapter recovers this knowledge (an oracle gap of $+0.283$ over the base model). However, the stored knowledge is not recoverable by similarity: given a query with no subgraph, embedding-based and weight-space geometry retrieval both perform at chance, because a semantically neighbouring entity's adapter does not contain the answer - knowledge is stored locally and does not transfer. Weight geometry correlates with subgraph semantics ($ρ= +0.329$) but not with functional retrievability. We quantify the byte and context-token costs against graph retrieval-augmented generation and discuss deployment implications. Our results establish that parametric knowledge graph memory is feasible for storing knowledge, and identify selecting and composing the right adapters by a mechanism other than semantic similarity as the central open problem - motivating a learned, query-conditioned composition mechanism.

</details>


### [67] [Clearing the Underbrush: AI-Enhanced RF Interference Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24974)
*Rahul Jain,Pierre Trepagnier,Rick Gentile,Joey Botero,Alexia Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在基于自回归Transformer模型的AI干扰抑制方法基础上，引入有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层和多种推理优化技术，在保持低延迟的同时提升射频信号中结构化干扰（如数字电视OFDM信号）的抑制性能，实验结果优于传统方法和此前AI方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的结构化干扰抑制方法性能有限，而AI方法能联合考虑目标信号与混合信号实现更优表现。本文旨在进一步提升AI方法的干扰抑制性能，同时将推理延迟控制在最低水平，并探索推理加速优化策略。

Method: 在已有的基于自回归Transformer模型的AI干扰抑制框架上，新增有限标量量化（FSQ）分词器层以改善干扰抑制效果；同时尝试多种推理优化技术来加速推理过程。实验中目标信号为数字调制射频信号，结构化干扰为数字电视信号（OFDM传输）。

Result: 所提出的方法在保持低延迟的同时，干扰抑制性能优于传统方法和此前其他AI方法；通过PESQ等音频指标验证了AI方法的优势，并展示了多种操作相关场景中的应用。

Conclusion: FSQ分词器层结合推理优化技术能够有效提升AI干扰抑制的性能与效率，该方法在多种实际应用场景中具有可行性和实用性。

Abstract: AI-based structured interference rejection has grown more popular because deep learning approaches can outperform traditional methods by jointly considering the signal of interest (SOI) and the signal mixture (SOI plus interference). This work builds on a previous AI-enabled approach utilizing autoregressive transformer-based models by adding a Finite Scalar Quantization (FSQ) tokenizer layer which aims to improve the interference rejection performance while keeping overall latency to a minimum. Additionally, we experiment with other inference optimization techniques with the goal of speeding up inference without much accuracy loss. We explore this space with an experiment where the SOI is a digitally modulated radio frequency (RF) signal and the structured interference is a digital television signal, an extremely common type of Orthogonal Frequency-Division Multiplexing (OFDM) transmission. Our results achieve low latency and increased interference rejection over traditional techniques and prior work with other AI-enabled methods. We demonstrate the benefits of the AI-enabled approaches via audio metrics such as Perceptual Evaluation of Speech Quality (PESQ). Additionally, we explore a variety of applications and detail how our interference rejection algorithm may be used in operationally-relevant scenarios.

</details>


### [68] [DCEO: Direct Causal Effect Optimization for Long-Term User Value Modeling in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25635)
*Junzhao Zhang,Tao Zhang,Liren Yu,Feiyi Dong,Zhixuan Zhang,Dan Ou,Haihong Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCEO（直接因果效应优化）框架，用于学习与用户级长期目标（如GMV）更好对齐的商品级代理分数，通过actor-critic框架动态生成上下文相关的多目标融合权重，替代传统手工设计的权重融合方案，在工业电商搜索系统中部署并取得显著线上收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索系统的最终目标是用户级长期目标（如N天累计购买、GMV），但搜索排序基于请求内的商品级分数，两者存在粒度鸿沟。现有方法通过手工设计的多目标融合（点击、加购、购买、交易额）来桥接这一差距，但手工融合依赖少量人工调参的权重，缺乏细粒度个性化，与最终目标的对齐效果不佳。

Method: DCEO首先将商品级代理分数聚合成用户级代理指标，并用相对因果效应量化其与最终目标的对齐程度。然后构建actor-critic框架：critic估计给定用户级代理指标下的最终目标，actor动态生成上下文相关的多目标融合权重以构建商品级代理分数，并通过直接优化相对因果效应对actor进行训练。

Result: 离线实验验证了DCEO的有效性和可解释性，并已部署于大规模工业电商搜索系统，在41天在线A/B测试中相较传统GMV代理在GMV上提升了0.36%。

Conclusion: DCEO通过数据驱动的方式学习与最终长期目标更好对齐的商品级代理分数，克服了手工权重融合的局限，实现了更细粒度的个性化排序，并在实际工业系统中验证了其显著效果。

Abstract: Industrial e-commerce search systems ultimately aim to optimize the user-level long-term objective, such as n-day cumulative purchases or gross merchandise value (GMV) per user. However, such objectives are defined at the user level, whereas search ranking is based on item-level scores within each request. Existing methods typically bridge this granularity gap through manually designed multi-objective fusion, where predictions of multiple item-level objectives, such as clicks, carts, purchases, and transaction value, are combined into a ranking score that serves as a proxy for the ultimate objective.
